* *Control windows*. Every "never" check has a control in the same leaf. The control drives the same signal where a healthy design drives it, and the leaf fails if the control does not show the signal. A leaf with zero PASS lines for a required check fails.
* *Log shape*. `CHK-<NAME> PASS|FAIL seed=<n> key=value ...`. A PASS line prints the expected and the observed value. PASS and FAIL come from `results.xml` evidence of an executed comparison. A clean simulator exit is not evidence.
* *Image rules*. The OTP image is staged through the preload. A leaf has one t=0 image and never restages it. The pre-sim hook `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/dv_sim_prestage.py` builds that image from the leaf seed before the simulator starts, so the image dimensions (the lifecycle state code, the lock layout, the field values and the disable vectors) are seed draws, and the leaf logs each of them as a `DRAW` line. A cold reset senses the same bank again, with every program of the leaf kept. Every 256-bit field has eight distinct words that are non-zero and not all ones. Reserved and unused bits stay 0. Lock bits stay 0 unless the test draws them. `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` follow the vector filter unless the entry states an exception and its reason.
* *Vector filter*. A pair of disable vectors is legal for a leaf when, in each of DBG_1 (bits 0 to 23), DBG_2 (bits 24 to 47) and Function (bits 48 to 63), the OR of the two vectors has at least one 1 and at least one 0, and each group has at least one bit set in only one vector. Without this filter a demotion, an owner veto or an RMA bypass has the same result as no effect, and the check cannot fail.
* *Time*. The specification gives no cycle count for sense, hash or program. Steps are placed by a probe or by a status poll with a bound. No wait for a program or a read request ends on a busy bit, because nothing shows that busy has risen before the first poll. Such a wait lasts a window W, a test-parameter multiple of the sense time measured in the leaf, with no early exit, and the status is read after it. A window that proves "never" is bounded by a multiple of the sense time measured in a control sense of the same leaf.
* *Persistence*. OTP bits, token arrays, the retained digest and its valid bit survive cold reset (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: reset alone does not revoke a prior token authorization). Every epoch that matches a token ends with a mismatch go on each matched channel before its cold reset, unless the entry grades the retained match. A SEC_DIS match never carries into the next leaf.
* *Digest staging*. The hash input is the 32-byte big-endian value of `token[255:0]`, index 7 first, no byte swap, and the comparison is `SHA256(token[255:0]) == expected_digest[255:0]` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission). The test reads the SHA-256 output as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, first digest byte most significant, and stages each RMA digest as that value in the 256-bit `token_digest` field with shadow word k holding bits [32k+31:32k], the same word rule that the token arrays state (index 0 holds `token[31:0]`). The specification states no split of the digest into shadow words and bytes (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Digest word and byte order), so this staging is a stimulus choice and no check grades it. No leaf takes a packing from the DUT. The first Match step of a leaf that stages a digest is the control of the staging: a leaf whose control step does not read Match fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and its later steps are not graded.
* *SEC_DIS token*. The expected SEC_DIS digest is a constant bound at the bench (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). The bench binds the digest of the all-zero token, so `TOKEN_ZERO` is the SEC_DIS right token: a test presents it only to activate SEC_DIS, and every other SEC_DIS go carries `TOKEN_OTHER`. A leaf that makes a SEC_DIS go starts with `CHK-BIND` and logs `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h>`: the SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the bound digest. SEC_DIS acts only while its revision enable has the A0 value 1 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Silicon Revision Scope). No leaf sets that value, because the carrier of it is open (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The first SEC_DIS match of every leaf is the control of the assumption: `security_disable_o` must rise, or the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
* *Strap during a token go*. Every SEC_DIS token go runs with `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. The specification says that test enable freezes the retained digest latches and does not say which signal is test enable, so no test of this bucket grades a token match with the strap at 1 (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Test enable and token freeze).
* *Bit address*. A program or read bit address is 8 times the shadow byte offset of the field plus the bit index (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields; `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`).
* *Testlist*. Each test is one `[[tests]]` entry of `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml` with `run_modes = ["no_cpu"]`, `target = "lsu_stub_all_live"` and its seed count, which stays small (3 or 4). No test has a per-cell testlist variant, and no plusarg pins a cell. Regression seeds are random per leaf, and a leaf does not know its run index, so no seed is tied to a cell. A cell that needs its own image, its own strap or its own leaf is a seed draw (weighted toward the corners where the entry says so), and its coverage closes over a merge of regressions (`SEP_FCOV.adoc`, Seed-dependent groups). No statement says that one regression fills a drawn cell. A leaf walks several cells only where the walk is cheap: register-level cells with no extra boot and no extra cold reset. A cheap cell that is contract-critical (a legal boundary, one representative per leg, the error injection itself) runs on every seed. The tests are members of the groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. A test that drives the JTAG `sep_reset_n` override, on the bench pins or through the IC_RESET TDR, adds the assertion waiver plusarg `+sep_edn_jtag_reset_waive` (<<sep-vplan-phase3-edn-waiver>>). No test needs the full CPU model.

'''''
[[sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test]]
===== 6.18 `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. The rows of `CHK-CSR-RESET` with a zero reset value count as evidence on VCS only, because a 2-state run reads 0 from an unreset flop.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor), with the response code of every access. The port `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the sensed-shadow probe `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. A register model written from the register descriptions `efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, `efuse_mmr.adoc` and `sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. Every probe is approved. The test needs no inject and no PROPOSED addition.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RANDCFG). The draws are: two 28-bit timeout counts (non-zero, not `0x800000`, enable bit 0); the bits 18, 16 and 15:0 of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and the bits 15:0 of `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` (non-zero patterns; every go bit and every enable bit stays 0); the word order of each of the three token arrays; seven past-extent offsets in `0x20` to `0xFC` of the interface-control window (word aligned); six reserved-span addresses in `0x1093_0604` to `0x1093_FFF8` (word aligned), one of them in `0x1093_0604` to `0x1093_0FFC` and one in `0x1093_1000` to `0x1093_FFF8`. Alias targets: at least one of the seven offsets is `0x20` or `0x24` (a `SYS_DIS` word), and at least one drawn address in each reserved zone has bits [9:0] at a software-writable shadow word (byte offset `0x0`, `0x4`, `0x8`, `0x18`, `0x1C`, `0x20`, `0x24`, `0x1B8` or `0x1BC`, the registers with access RW in `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`). The word index w of the control write (0 or 1, a `SYS_DIS` word). `reseed = 4` samples four sets of these draws per regression; no bin depends on a drawn offset or address, so more sets come from later regressions and no cell waits on them. These cells run on every seed: the reset reads, the control reads, the control write, and the pinned addresses `0x1093_041C`, `0x1093_0600`, `0x1093_0C1C` and `0x1093_FFFC`. The pinned addresses `0x1093_041C` and `0x1093_0C1C` have bits [9:0] at `0x1C`, the high word of `SIP_DIS` (access RW).

*Objective*: After sense, the interface-control window (`0x1093_0400`, 28 bytes) and the token window (`0x1093_0500`) hold the described registers with their reset values and read-write behaviour. No access past the interface-control extent, and no access in the reserved span at `0x1093_0600`, changes `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` or `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, or aliases a software-writable shadow word, after a real sense. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/memory_map.adoc` (rows eFuse Interface Control, eFuse Token Registers, Reserved); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (token arrays, `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc` (register `FEAT_CTRL`). Past the interface-control extent the APB decode answers SLVERR (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section OTP Fuse Controller, preamble). The test logs that response as a control and does not grade it, because `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades it. The response code and read data columns of `memory_map.adoc` state the view at the inbound AXI port (`hw/sys/sep/doc/memory_map.adoc`, address map preamble). `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` sends addresses past the last register window to the shim bank control registers. So no cited source states the CPU-side response of the reserved span, and none states its read data. The test logs that response and that read data and does not grade them. For the reserved span it grades only that `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and the 256 shadow words are unchanged after each access. The specification states no CPU-side read data past the interface-control extent (`0x1093_041C` to `0x1093_04FF`), so a refused read there can legally return any value. The test logs that read data and does not grade it, and it does not grade the exact code past the extent (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). A legal write to a set-only shadow word (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, row `SYS_DIS`: set-only, software writable; `sep_efuse_map.adoc`, register `SYS_DIS`, access RW1S) is the control that a write to a shadow word is visible.

*Test Coverage*: Reset values of the five interface-control registers after a real sense. OKAY reads of the two interface read-data registers. Write and readback of the writable fields of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with every go and enable bit at 0. Write and readback of all 24 token words of the three arrays in a drawn word order. The write and readback of both timeout counts with enable 0 and the reset value of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` are logged controls, because `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades them. A write to the read-only status bits and to the read-only `FEAT_CTRL`. Eight past-extent accesses (the pinned offset `0x1C` and seven drawn offsets) and the reserved-span accesses (the pinned addresses `0x1093_0600`, `0x1093_0C1C` and `0x1093_FFFC` and six drawn addresses, at least one in each reserved zone), each read and each written, with the no-change compare of the three registers and, where bits [9:0] of the address select a software-writable shadow word, of that shadow word. One legal write of the complement of a `SYS_DIS` word, read back as all ones, as the control that a write to a shadow word is visible. The token-window span past `0x1093_0573` is not drawn, because `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` and `memory_map.adoc` give different codes for it (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The test extends three open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` keeps the refusal, the response code and the read data past the interface-control extent, the no-change of the two timeouts there, the timeout write and readback, the write and readback of one word of two token arrays, and the reset value of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, all run without a real sense. `sep_axi_map_refuse_test` keeps the response of the reserved span. `sep_efuse_image_test` keeps the shadow words after sense. This entry adds the reset values of the five interface-control registers after a real sense, the read-write behaviour of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, the write and readback of all 24 token words, and the no-change of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and the no-alias onto a shadow word of the past-extent and reserved accesses after a real sense. The response past the interface-control extent is logged as a control and is not graded. The open owners keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_map_geometry_cg` (`cp_window`, `cp_rsvd_zone`, `x_win_op_resp`, `cp_edge_addr`, `x_ctrl_reg_op`, and the token array cells of `x_token_blk_op`; the `TOKEN_EOP` write cell and the three match read cells belong to `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`). The logged controls (the response past the extent, the timeout writes, the match reads and the `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` reads) credit no bin.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed. Print `SEED seed=<n>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Stage the OTP image through the preload: TEST_DEV (`LC_STATE` word `0xF0`); `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` a filter-legal pair; each 256-bit field its own fixed vector; every lock bit 0; the other fields by the image rules. The staged vector words differ from 0, from all ones and from `0xBADCAB1E` (the read data that `memory_map.adoc` lists for an unmapped read at the inbound port). Every software-writable shadow word has at least one RW1S bit at 0 in the staged image (the vector filter admits only a pair with a 0 bit in each 32-bit word of `SIP_DIS` and of `SYS_DIS`, and `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and `REQUIRED_ALGS` have a used bit at 0), so a write of the complement that aliases onto the word changes it.
. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Reset reads. Before any write of the leaf, read through the frontdoor, in this order: `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` (`0x1093_0400`), `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` (`0x1093_0404`), `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` (`0x1093_0408`), `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` (`0x1093_0414`), `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` (`0x1093_0418`) and `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` (`0x1093_0570`).
. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` (`0x1093_040C`) and `EFUSE_READ_INTERFACE_READ_DATA` (`0x1093_0410`). Log the response and the data. Do not grade the data.
. Read the three match registers (`0x1093_0564`, `0x1093_0568`, `0x1093_056C`). Log the values. Do not grade them: the retained digest and its valid bit survive reset and have no reset value. The leaf issues no go. Read no token array before its first write, because the arrays have no reset.
. Write the two drawn timeout counts with enable 0: `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT`, then `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`. Read each register back.
. Write the drawn pattern to `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` and `efuse_program_go` at 0. Read the register back. Write the drawn pattern to `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` and `efuse_read_go` at 0. Read the register back.
. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write of `0x0000_0071` to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` (bits 0, 4, 5 and 6 at 1, the three clear bits at 0). Kind: write of 1 to read-only fields. Expected response: the register reads `0x1` after the write. The response code is not graded. Read the register.
. Write `TOKEN_T1` to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0500`), `TOKEN_T2` to `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0520`) and `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0540`). Write the eight words of each array in its drawn order. Issue no go. Read the eight words of each array back, then read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` again.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` (`0x1091_8000`, 64 bit) through the frontdoor.
. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write to `FEAT_CTRL`, as two 32-bit writes of the bitwise complement of the value of step 11 (low word, then high word). Kind: write to a read-only register. Expected response: the register value is unchanged. The response code is not graded. Read `FEAT_CTRL` again.
. Control reads. Read the shadow word at `0x1093_03FC` and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` at `0x1093_0400`. Both answer OKAY.
. Past-extent leg. For each address in the order `0x1093_041C` (pinned), then the seven drawn offsets: read the three graded interface-control registers (STATUS, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`) and the shadow word at address bits [9:0]; read the address and record the response and the data; read the same registers and word again; write the bitwise complement of that shadow word to the address and record the response; read the same registers and word a third time. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor read and write at the address. Kind: unmapped address past the interface-control extent. Expected response: no change of a register or a word. The read data and the response code are logged and not graded.
. Reserved leg. Run the loop of step 14 on `0x1093_0600` (pinned), `0x1093_0C1C` (pinned), `0x1093_FFFC` (pinned) and the six drawn addresses. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor read and write at the address. Kind: unmapped address in the reserved span. Expected response: no change of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` or a shadow word. The read data and the response code are logged and not graded.
. Repeat the control reads of step 13.
. Control write. Take the drawn word w of `SYS_DIS` (byte offset `0x20` plus 4 times w, shadow address `0x1093_0000` plus that offset). Write the bitwise complement of its staged value to it through the frontdoor. This is a legal shadow write: every lock bit is 0, `secure_tm_req_i` is 0 and the leaf runs in TEST_DEV. Read the word back; the OR-merge of the staged value and its complement is all ones. Set the golden of that word to all ones for the next step.
. Read the 256 shadow words through the frontdoor.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-CSR-RESET
|After sense done, each of these registers answers OKAY and reads its described reset value: `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` `0x00000001` (`efuse_sense_done` 1, the error flags and clear bits 0); `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` `0x00000000`; `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` `0x00800000` (enable 0, cycles `0x800000`) (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, reset column of each field; for the sense-done flag, which has no reset value, the description of `efuse_sense_done`, bit 0, and `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the sense-completion indicator). The two read-data registers answer OKAY, and their data is logged and not graded. A DUT with a wrong reset value, a missing sense-done bit or a stuck error flag fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of each register. Control: the status read comes after `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is high, so a clear sense-done bit is a DUT fault and not a read that came too early. On Verilator only the rows with a non-zero reset value count; the zero rows log `OBS-CSR-RESET LOG` and no `PASS` field. Evidence: `CHK-CSR-RESET PASS seed=<n> reg=<name> exp=0x<v> got=0x<v>` (one line per register); `OBS-CSR-DATA LOG seed=<n> reg=<name> resp=OKAY rdata=0x<v>` (the two read-data registers).
|CHK-CSR-RW
|Each write to a writable register answers OKAY, and the register reads back the value written: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` bits 18, 16 and 15:0 (with `program_enable` and `efuse_program_go` at 0, and the status bits 26:24 reading 0), `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` bits 15:0 (with `read_enable` and `efuse_read_go` at 0, and the status bits 26:24 reading 0). The drawn patterns are non-zero, so a read of the reset value cannot equal the written value (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, the access column of each field). A write of `0x71` to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` leaves the register at `0x00000001`: the status bits are read only, and the response code of that write is not graded. The 28-bit cycle count of each timeout register (enable at 0) reads back the value written; that row is a logged control with no `PASS` field, because `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades the timeout write and readback. A DUT with a stuck or unwritable field, a writable status bit, or an error response to a legal write fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of the field bits of each register; the reserved bits are logged. Control: the reset-value reads of CHK-CSR-RESET come before the first write, so each readback differs from the reset value for the written fields. Evidence: `CHK-CSR-RW PASS seed=<n> reg=<name> wr=0x<v> got=0x<v> resp=OKAY`; `CHK-CSR-RW PASS seed=<n> reg=STATUS wr=0x00000071 got=0x00000001`; `OBS-CSR-RW LOG seed=<n> reg=<timeout register> wr=0x<v> got=0x<v>`.
|CHK-TOKEN-RW
|Each token array returns the eight words written to it in the drawn order, and each write answers OKAY (`efuse_mmr.adoc`, registers `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I`, `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_I`, `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I`). `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` writes and reads back one word of two arrays, and this check adds the walk of all 24 words. `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` is read before and after the token writes, because no go is issued, and is logged as a control with no `PASS` field, because that test grades its reset value (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: reset clears the sticky fault flags). The match register values before any go are logged and not graded. A DUT whose array loses a word or swaps two words fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of each word. Control: the three arrays hold three different vectors, so a read that returns the word of another array differs. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-RW PASS seed=<n> arr=<sip/chp/sdis> words=8 equal=1`; `OBS-TOKEN-FAULT LOG seed=<n> fault_before=0x<v> fault_after=0x<v> match_logged=0x<v>`.
|CHK-FEAT-RO
|`FEAT_CTRL` reads the same 64-bit value before and after a write of its bitwise complement (`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`, register `FEAT_CTRL`, access R). A DUT whose `FEAT_CTRL` takes a bus write fails. The response code of the write is not graded. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the written value differs from the value read, because it is the complement. Evidence: `CHK-FEAT-RO PASS seed=<n> before=0x<64b> wr=0x<64b> after=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-WIN-DECODE
|After every access past the interface-control extent and in the reserved span, `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and the shadow word at address bits [9:0] read the values of the model: STATUS `0x00000001` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, description of `efuse_sense_done`, bit 0; `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the sense-completion indicator), the two control registers the values that step 8 wrote to them (the drawn patterns, with the go and enable bits 0), and the shadow word its staged value. The shadow-word compare is graded only where bits [9:0] of the address select a software-writable shadow word (access RW in `sep_efuse_map.adoc`), which has an RW1S bit at 0 in the staged image, because a write cannot change a word with access R; at every other address the shadow word is read and logged (`alias_graded=0`) and the register compare stays graded. Every leg has graded alias targets: the pinned addresses `0x1093_041C` and `0x1093_0C1C` and the alias targets of the draw. The read data of every access past the interface-control extent and in the reserved span is logged and not graded: the specification states no CPU-side read data past the extent (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, CPU-side response at the eFuse interface-control holes), and the reserved span goes to the shim bank control registers, whose contents the adopter defines. At the end of the leaf all 256 shadow words equal their staged values, except the word of step 17, which equals all ones. A DUT that wraps the address of a write onto a shadow word or a register, or takes the write, fails. The response of each access past the extent and in the reserved span is logged as `OBS-WIN-RESP LOG` and is not graded here (`sep_unmapped_access_policy_test` grades the refusal past the extent; the reserved span has no stated CPU-side response). Anchor: the frontdoor readback of the registers and words. The golden is the register model (reset value and the values the leaf wrote) and the staged image, not a value read from the DUT before the access. Control: a write of the same kind is visible. The legal writes of step 8 to `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` read back (CHK-CSR-RW), and the legal write of step 17, the complement of a `SYS_DIS` word, reads back as all ones (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, set-only OR-merge), so an alias write onto a register or onto a shadow word would show. The shadow word at `0x1093_03FC` and the register at `0x1093_0400` answer OKAY with the staged value and the reset value, before the first unmapped access and after the last one. Evidence: `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=<ctrl/rsvd> addr=0x<a> op=<rd/wr> wdata=0x<v> alias_graded=<1\|0> alias_exp=0x<v> alias_got=0x<v> regs_equal=1`; `OBS-WIN-RESP LOG seed=<n> win=<ctrl/rsvd> addr=0x<a> op=<rd/wr> resp=<code> rdata=0x<v>`; `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=ctl addr=0x<a> op=rd resp=OKAY got=0x<v> exp=0x<v>`; `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=ctlwr word=<w> addr=0x<a> staged=0x<v> wr=0x<v> got=0xffffffff`; `CHK-WIN-DECODE PASS seed=<n> win=sweep words=256 mismatches=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not grade the response past the interface-control extent (OKAY or not, and the exact code), the response code and the read data of the reserved span, the response code of a write to a read-only register, or the data of the two interface read-data registers. The token-window span past `0x1093_0573` is not drawn. A match result and the effect of a go belong to `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`. The refusal, the response code and the read data past the extent, and the no-change of the two timeouts there, belong to `sep_unmapped_access_policy_test`, the response of the reserved span to `sep_axi_map_refuse_test`, and the shadow words after sense to `sep_efuse_image_test`. A zero reset value is graded on VCS only.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test]]
===== 6.19 `sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one fuse image, so the seed draws the field values of that image (the preload hook stages it from the seed), and each leaf reads one value per field.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor). The port `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the sensed-shadow probe `efuse_shadow_probe_o` (approved). The OTP image preload (approved). The fixed vectors for the 256-bit fields. An offset table that the test builds from the address column of `sep_efuse_map.adoc`. Added hook, a top-port drive that the test adds to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`: a drive of `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, which `tb_top` ties to 0, for CHK-JTAGID. The DUT port `jtag_id_i` is read as an observation tap. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The draws are: the value of each narrow field; the choice of the fixed vector of each 256-bit field (no two fields share a vector); the write-lock set of `LOCKS_SPARE`; and `jtag_id_i[31:1]`. Each graded narrow field draws its class first, with equal weight per class, and then a value inside the class, so the edge values take most of the draws: `SBOOT_DIS` 0 or 1, and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 1 - `SBOOT_DIS` (the test presents no token, so no update follows); `STATUS_RPT`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and each nibble of `REQUIRED_ALGS` (chiplet, SiP, system) one of 0 to 3; `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ` one of {1, 2047, a value in 2 to 2046}; `ROM_CTL` endianness 0 or 1, and swap field one of {`0b00000`, each single bit (five classes), `0b11111`, another value}; `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` one of {0, one bit of bits 25:0, all of bits 25:0, two or more bits of bits 25:0 and not all}. `LOCKS` is a drawn non-zero set of write-lock bits (even bits 2 to 62, slots 1 to 31, so that slot 0 stays clear), with the two words different. `LOCKS_SPARE` is a drawn non-zero set of write-lock bits (even bits 0 to 16, slots 32 to 40). `LC_STATE` is TEST_DEV (word `0xF0`). `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` are a filter-legal pair. Trap filters: every two adjacent staged words differ (an equal pair redraws one of its words, a 256-bit vector choice first, else a narrow value inside its drawn class); no staged word equals any of the three 32-bit forms of `jtag_id_i`; every reserved bit is 0. `reseed = 4` gives four images per regression. The edge classes are seed-dependent cells: the coverage of `sep_efuse_field_value_cg` closes over a merge of regressions, about 4 regressions for the eight swap classes of `ROM_CTL`, the largest class list (8 ln 8 / 4).

*Objective*: The used-bit ranges of nine narrow registers read back as staged, at edge values that the seed draws: the ten 32-bit registers except `LOCKS_SPARE`, which is a lock word whose lock bits belong to `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`. The graded nine are `LC_STATE`, `SBOOT_DIS`, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `STATUS_RPT`, `ROM_CTL`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and `REQUIRED_ALGS`. The register layout is a control: the shadow map holds 43 registers at the described byte offsets and widths, which are ten 32-bit registers (the nine above and `LOCKS_SPARE`), three 64-bit registers (`LOCKS`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`) and thirty 256-bit registers, and the 96-bit `LOCK` field spans `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE`. A drawn non-zero `jtag_id_i` does not enter any shadow word: the JTAG ID is not in the fuse map and is a top-level port (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, the text after the field table). The word-by-word compare of all 43 registers is a control, because `sep_efuse_image_test`, `sep_efuse_sense_test` and `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` grade it. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Fields, field table and the text after it); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` (section Configuration Parameters: 256 words of 32 bits); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (address map and the registers `LOCKS_SPARE`, `LC_STATE`, `SBOOT_DIS`, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`, `STATUS_RPT`, `ROM_CTL`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `SEP_CHIPLET_ID`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (port `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, for the port only).

*Test Coverage*: The offset and width of all 43 registers, from the RDL offset column, as a control: the table must tile the 256 words before any DUT access, and the word-by-word compare is logged and not graded. The used bits of the nine graded narrow registers (the ten 32-bit registers except `LOCKS_SPARE`), with values drawn from these edge classes over a merge of regressions: `SBOOT_DIS` 0 and 1; `STATUS_RPT`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` and each `REQUIRED_ALGS` nibble 0 to 3; `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ` 1, 2047 and a middle value; `ROM_CTL` endianness 0 and 1, and the swap field at 0, each single bit, all five bits and one other value; `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` 0, one bit, bits 25:0 all set and other values. The reserved bits of the narrow fields, logged. The absence of a drawn non-zero `jtag_id_i` in the 256 shadow words. The test extends two open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_efuse_image_test` compares the shadow field by field against the image golden after sense, and `sep_efuse_sense_test` senses an image. This entry adds the used-bit compare of the narrow fields with edge values and the absence of `jtag_id_i` in the shadow. The lock exemption of `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` belongs to `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`, and the end of the map to `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`. The word-by-word compare of the 43 registers is logged as `OBS-FIELD LOG` control lines and is not graded here. The open owners keep that grade. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_map_geometry_cg` (`cp_shadow_field`, and the non-zero bin of `cp_jtag_id_class`) and `sep_efuse_field_value_cg` (seed-dependent: one leaf fills one class per field, and the edge bins close over a merge of regressions).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Parse the address column of the `sep_efuse_map.adoc` address map into an offset table, and the widths from the field tables of the same file. The table is the only source of the offsets. Require 43 rows that tile the words 0 to 255 with no gap and no overlap. A table that does not tile fails the leaf before any DUT access with `SETUP FAIL reason=table_tile`. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed, each narrow field with its class. Print `SEED seed=<n>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value, with the class of each narrow field.
. Build the image. Every 256-bit field takes its own fixed vector. The narrow fields take the drawn values. Reserved and unused bits stay 0. Every lock bit is 0 except the drawn write-lock sets of `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE`. The leaf makes no program and no shadow write, so a write-lock changes no read. Check the adjacent rule on the 256 staged words. Redraw one word of an equal pair by the trap filter. If no redraw separates the pair, fail the leaf with `SETUP FAIL reason=adjacent_equal` before the first read.
. Draw `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, non-zero, so that none of its three 32-bit forms equals a staged word. Drive it from reset release to the end of the leaf.
. Preload the image. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Read every register through the frontdoor, word by word, at its table offset: the 43 registers cover words 0 to 255. Log each word with its staged value as `OBS-FIELD LOG seed=<n> field=<name> off=0x<o> words=<k> exp=0x<hex> got=0x<hex>` (one line per register, 43 lines). This is a control and is not graded. For a 32-bit narrow register the logged compare covers the bits of the used mask only: `LOCKS_SPARE` `0x0003FFFF`, `LC_STATE` `0x000000FF`, `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` `0x1`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` `0x03FFFFFF`, `STATUS_RPT` and `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` `0x3`, `ROM_CTL` `0x3F`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ` `0x7FF`, `REQUIRED_ALGS` `0x333`.
. Read each of the nine graded narrow registers again as one 32-bit access. Split the word into its used bits and its reserved bits. Compare the used bits with the staged value. Log the reserved bits. `LC_STATE` is among the nine; `LOCKS_SPARE` is not read again.
. Sample the DUT port `jtag_id_i` and the 256 sensed words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. The port must equal the drawn ID (hook control). Compare each word with the three 32-bit forms of `jtag_id_i`: `{id, 1'b0}`, `{id, 1'b1}` and `{1'b0, id}`. With the same compare, compare each word with the staged word of its index, with `LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` (positive control of the compare).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-FIELD-USED
|The used bits of each of the nine graded narrow registers (`LC_STATE`, `SBOOT_DIS`, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`, `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE`, `STATUS_RPT`, `ROM_CTL`, `SYSCLK_FREQ_MHZ`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`, `REQUIRED_ALGS`) equal the staged value, in a separate 32-bit read of the word at its table offset (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields; `sep_efuse_map.adoc`, address map). The reserved bits are logged and not graded (`sep_efuse_map.adoc`, the bit table of each narrow register). The 256-bit, 64-bit and `LOCKS_SPARE` words are not graded here, and the lock exemption of `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` belongs to `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`. A DUT that drops a used bit, shifts a field inside its word or reads a used bit as 0 for an edge value fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback, split into used and reserved bits. Control: in every leaf one of `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` is staged at 1 and the staged `LC_STATE` byte is `0xF0`, so a read of 0 differs; the class draw gives the edge values (0 and 1, the lowest and highest value of the used range, the single bits of `ROM_CTL`, bits 25:0 all set) most of the draws, so over a merge of regressions each used bit is read at 1 and at 0 and an unwritten or stuck bit shows. Evidence: nine lines per run, one per graded register, `CHK-FIELD-USED PASS seed=<n> field=<name> class=<c> used_mask=0x<m> exp=0x<v> got=0x<v> rsvd_got=0x<v>`; a run with fewer than nine lines fails.
|CHK-JTAGID
|No sensed shadow word equals any of the three 32-bit forms of the drawn non-zero ID: `{id, 1'b0}`, `{id, 1'b1}` and `{1'b0, id}` (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields: the JTAG_ID field is not in the fuse map and is a top-level port, so no shadow field takes it; `port_table.adoc`, port `jtag_id_i[31:1]`, for the port only). A DUT that stores the ID in a shadow word at any of these alignments fails. Anchor: the 256 words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and the frontdoor readback of step 6. Control: the DUT port `jtag_id_i` equals the drawn ID during the reads, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` (hook control); the trap filter makes all three forms differ from every staged word; and the same compare finds each of the 256 sensed words equal to the staged word of its index (`LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`), so the compare can see an equal word. Evidence: `CHK-JTAGID PASS seed=<n> id=0x<v> port=0x<v> f0=0x<v> f1=0x<v> fz=0x<v> words_checked=256 equal=0 staged_equal=256`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0. A leaf whose DUT port `jtag_id_i` does not equal the drawn ID fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.

*Scope limit*: The test makes no program, no shadow write and no lock write. It does not grade the reserved bits of a narrow field, the meaning of a field value, the lock behaviour or the access policy of a field. The value of every field against the image, and the word-by-word compare of the 43 registers, belong to `sep_efuse_image_test`, and the sense sequence to `sep_efuse_sense_test`. The reset values of the interface-control registers, the end of the map and the response past it belong to `sep_efuse_window_decode_reset_test`. The `jtag_id_i` check does not grade the IDCODE path of the JTAG TAP.

'''''

[[sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test]]
===== 6.20 `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the nibble of that image (the preload hook stages it from the seed). The JTAG master is live in that target. Leg C runs only in a leaf whose drawn nibble is `0000` (TEST_DEV), after legs A and B on the same image. It uses the added error inject `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`, and the flag at `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o` is its hook control.

*Source Module*: `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor). The JTAG master on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o` (approved, leg C). The bench input `lc_sigint_inject_i` (approved), the ports `lc_sigint_err_o`, `security_disable_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, the probes `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, and the OTP image preload (all approved). The register `FEAT_CTRL` is read through the frontdoor. A profile function written for this test from the Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile table. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_ZERO` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. Added hook, for leg C (CHK-JTAG-SIGINT): the error inject `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i` at the decoder input of the JTAG lifecycle filter, which the test adds to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o`, the integrity-error flag at the decoder output of that filter, is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The approved `lc_sigint_inject_i` acts at the lifecycle decoder, and the leaf does not use it for a JTAG grade.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The draws are: the nibble n, one of the seven nibbles of the open-profile classes {`0000`, `0001`, `0010`, `0011`, `0110`, `0111`, `1000`}, with weight 2 for `0000` (the nibble that also runs leg C) and weight 1 for each of the others; the disable-vector pair; and, in leg C, the JTAG shadow-word offset, the control bit and the drawn bit of the `SIP_DIS` writes, the two timeout counts c1 and c2 (28-bit, non-zero, different, not `0x800000`) and the word order of the token write. The X bit of an RMA code is bit 0 of n and is never drawn. Trap filter: the vector filter (so the profile row of the control epoch is non-zero for every nibble); in leg C, the control bit and the drawn bit are two different bits that the staged `SIP_DIS` lacks (the vector filter leaves at least one 0 bit of `SIP_DIS` in each group). `reseed = 4` gives four nibbles per regression. The nibble is a seed-dependent cell: the seven nibbles of `x_sigint_state_secdis_out` close over a merge of regressions, about 4 regressions (7 ln 7 / 4). Leg C runs in a leaf that draws `0000` (weight 2 of 8), so its cells close over a merge of regressions.

*Objective*: A non-complementary byte at the lifecycle decoder input raises `lc_sigint_err_o` and forces `FEAT_CTRL` to 0, unless SEC_DIS holds, which forces all ones even on an integrity error. On an LC differential-decode integrity error the OTP JTAG wrapper admits only the MMR region, which holds the token inputs. The specification does not say that the wrapper takes the error from the decoder of the lifecycle controller, so leg C injects at the decoder of the JTAG filter. The OTP cannot hold such a byte, because the fuse sensing loads the raw nibble and stores it differentially encoded in the shadow. So the leaf injects the byte with the bench input `lc_sigint_inject_i`. The profile of the 16 raw codes (legal and INVALID) is a leg of `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Life Cycle Controller, Lifecycle States, Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS), Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile, Debug and Test Port Path Gating, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`; Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control); `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Implementation Requirements); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (port `lc_sigint_err_o`); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` (paragraph on the lifecycle state load, `HAS_LC_STATE`).

*Test Coverage*: Leg A: the injected non-complementary byte in each of the seven open-profile nibbles, after a control epoch of the same state and vector pair that shows a non-zero profile. Leg B: SEC_DIS over the injected error (a `TOKEN_ZERO` match), then a `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch. Leg C: the JTAG policy under the filter-side injection in a TEST_DEV image, with a control epoch without injection that reads two targets and writes one `SIP_DIS` bit and one interface-control register. The injection epoch covers every class of the policy: a shadow word read and write, an interface-control register read and write, a token word read and write, the `TOKEN_EOP` go and the match read. The nibble `0111` of leg A is the cell that `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` injects after its chain, so it runs as a control with `role=control` and is not credited as new coverage. The other six nibbles of leg A and every nibble of leg B are new cells. No check grades anything after the injection input falls, because the specification does not state a release of `lc_sigint_err_o`. The test extends two open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` walks the lifecycle to feature-control path and injects one integrity error through `lc_sigint_inject_i`. `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test` reads the shadow map and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` over JTAG in TEST_DEV and is the control of leg C. This entry adds the injected error in six open-profile nibbles (the nibble `0111` of leg A is a control), SEC_DIS over the integrity error in each of the seven, and the JTAG policy under the error. The open owners keep the rest. Phase 1 group `sep_lc_state_cg` takes hits. Functional coverage: `sep_lc_sigint_failclosed_cg` (seed-dependent: `x_sigint_state_secdis_out`, one drawn nibble per leaf, closed over a merge of regressions) and `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg` (the integrity-error bin of `cp_state` and the integrity-error cells of `x_state_class_op`, seed-dependent: they fill in a leaf that draws `0000`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed. Print `SEED seed=<n> n=<nibble>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Legs A and B. Run `CHK-BIND` first (the leaf makes a SEC_DIS go in leg B).
. Legs A and B. Stage `{~n, n}` as the `LC_STATE` byte and a filter-legal disable-vector pair. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Leg A, control epoch. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Sample `lc_sigint_err_o`. Expect the profile row of the class of n (non-zero, by the vector filter) and `lc_sigint_err_o` at 0.
. Leg A, injection epoch. Raise `lc_sigint_inject_i`. It forces both rails of the byte at the lifecycle decoder input to 0, a non-complementary byte. Error injection. Point: `lc_sigint_inject_i`. Kind: both rails of the byte at 0. This is the only corrupt value that the bench injects. Expected response: `lc_sigint_err_o` = 1 and `FEAT_CTRL` = 0.
. Leg A. Wait, bounded, for `lc_sigint_err_o` = 1. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Expect 0 in both.
. Leg B. With the input still raised, write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` (`0x1093_0540`) and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16 (`0x1093_0560`). Wait, bounded, until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` (`0x1093_056C`) reads `0x15`. `security_disable_o` rises. If it does not rise, fail the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Leg B. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor. Expect all ones. Sample `lc_sigint_err_o`; expect 1.
. Leg B. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16. Wait, bounded, until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`. The code before this go is the `0x15` of the previous step, so a go that has no effect times out the wait. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect 0 with `lc_sigint_err_o` still 1.
. Legs A and B. Lower `lc_sigint_inject_i`. Cold reset. Grade nothing after the input falls.
. Leg C. Leg C runs only in a leaf whose drawn nibble is `0000`: its image is the TEST_DEV image of legs A and B, with a filter-legal pair whose `SIP_DIS` has at least one 0 bit in each group, and a leaf with another nibble ends after step 10. When leg C runs, wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` after the cold reset of step 10.
. Leg C, control epoch (no injection). Read the drawn shadow word and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` over the JTAG master. Both answer OKAY. Sample `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o`; expect 0. Record the responses.
. Leg C, control epoch (no injection). Write 1 in the control bit of `SIP_DIS` over the JTAG master. The write answers OKAY. Read `SIP_DIS` (`0x1093_0018`) through the frontdoor. The control bit reads 1 and the drawn bit reads 0. Record the value. Write the count c1 with enable 0 to `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` (`0x1093_0414`) over the JTAG master. The write answers OKAY. Read the register through the frontdoor. It reads c1.
. Leg C, injection epoch. Error injection. Point: `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`. Kind: both rails of the byte at the decoder input of the JTAG filter at 0. Expected response: `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o` = 1. Raise the injection. Wait, bounded, for the probe to read 1. If it stays 0, fail the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` and grade no JTAG access. Sample `lc_sigint_err_o` and log it; it is not graded.
. Leg C. Read `SIP_DIS` through the frontdoor and record it. Repeat the two JTAG reads of the control epoch. Error injection. Point: the JTAG master. Kind: read of a shadow word and of an interface-control register while the filter error probe is 1. Expected response: a response that is not OKAY. The read data is not graded.
. Leg C. Write 1 in the drawn bit of `SIP_DIS` over the JTAG master. Error injection. Point: the JTAG master. Kind: write to a shadow register while the filter error probe is 1. Expected response: a response that is not OKAY and no change of `SIP_DIS` from the value recorded in the control epoch. Read `SIP_DIS` through the frontdoor.
. Leg C. Write the count c2 with enable 0 to `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` over the JTAG master. Error injection. Point: the JTAG master. Kind: write to an interface-control register while the filter error probe is 1. Expected response: a response that is not OKAY and no change of the register from c1. Read the register through the frontdoor.
. Leg C. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I` over the JTAG master, in the drawn word order. Read the first word written over the JTAG master. Write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 0. Read `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` over the JTAG master. Every access of this step answers OKAY. The read data is not graded.
. Leg C. Lower `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`. Cold reset.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|Per the common rules of this bucket: the SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the digest that the bench binds as the SEC_DIS expected digest (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A bench whose bound digest is not that of `TOKEN_ZERO` fails the leaf before any SEC_DIS go. Anchor: the bound constant at the bench against a SHA-256 of the 32 zero bytes. Control: none; the check grades the bench. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h>`.
|CHK-SIGINT
|The injected byte gives `lc_sigint_err_o` = 1 and `FEAT_CTRL` = 0 in each of the seven open-profile nibbles (the nibble `0111` in leg A is a control of the cell that `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` grades, logged with `role=control`) (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS): the integrity error forces the base feature vector to all 0s). A DUT with the integrity logic off keeps the non-zero profile and fails. Anchor: the `FEAT_CTRL` register readback and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, with the `lc_sigint_err_o` port. Control: the control epoch shows the non-zero profile row with `lc_sigint_err_o` low for the same state and vector pair; a control that differs from the row fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-SIGINT PASS seed=<n> n=0x<v> role=<new/control> exp_feat=0x0 got_feat=0x0 sigint=1 ctl_feat=0x<64b> ctl_exp=0x<64b> ctl_sigint=0`.
|CHK-SIGINT-SECDIS
|With the SEC_DIS match, `FEAT_CTRL` is all ones with `lc_sigint_err_o` still 1. After the `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch, `FEAT_CTRL` returns to 0, so the all-ones value comes from the override (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS): the override applies after the integrity fail-closed step and wins). A DUT that lets the integrity error win over SEC_DIS shows 0 and fails. A DUT that holds all ones after the mismatch fails. Anchor: the `FEAT_CTRL` register readback, the ports `security_disable_o` and `lc_sigint_err_o`, and the SEC_DIS match register. Control: `security_disable_o` rises on the match, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and the 0 before the match (CHK-SIGINT) is the contrast. Evidence: `CHK-SIGINT-SECDIS PASS seed=<n> n=0x<v> got_feat=0xffffffffffffffff sigint=1 sdis=1 after_mismatch=0x0 code_match=0x15 code_mismatch=0x2a`.
|CHK-JTAG-SIGINT
|Leg C, in a leaf that draws the nibble `0000`. Graded only when the injection is shown to reach the decoder of the JTAG filter: `jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o` is 1 in the injection epoch. While the filter error probe is 1, a JTAG read of a shadow word and of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` answers a response that is not OKAY. The JTAG write to `SIP_DIS` answers a response that is not OKAY, and the frontdoor read of `SIP_DIS` equals its value from the control epoch, with the drawn bit still 0. The JTAG write of c2 to `EFUSE_READ_REQ_TIMEOUT` answers a response that is not OKAY, and the frontdoor read of it equals c1. The token word write, the token word read, the go and the match read answer OKAY (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Debug and Test Port Path Gating, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path: on an LC differential-decode integrity error the wrapper admits only the MMR region for reads and writes; the specification does not state that the wrapper uses the decoder of the lifecycle controller). The read data is not graded. A DUT that keeps the TEST_DEV policy under the error fails. Anchor: the JTAG response codes, the filter error probe and the frontdoor readback of `SIP_DIS`. Control: the control epoch, with the probe at 0, answers OKAY to the two reads (the open JTAG window test grades that cell) and answers OKAY to the JTAG write of the control bit and to the JTAG write of c1, and the frontdoor reads show that bit set and c1 in the register, so a JTAG write that never lands cannot pass the unchanged compares. A leaf whose injection epoch has the probe at 0 fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` (hook control). A leaf with another nibble logs `CHK-JTAG-SIGINT N/A seed=<n> n=0x<v>`. Evidence: one line per access of the injection epoch, `CHK-JTAG-SIGINT PASS seed=<n> filter_err=1 tgt=<class> op=<rd/wr> exp=<allow/deny> resp=<code> sip_dis_before=0x<64b> sip_dis_after=0x<64b> ctl_resp=OKAY ctl_wr_resp=OKAY ctl_bit_set=1 ctl_cw_resp=OKAY ctl_cw_landed=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0. A leaf that draws the nibble `0000` requires a CHK-JTAG-SIGINT PASS line for each access of the injection epoch; a leaf with another nibble requires the CHK-JTAG-SIGINT N/A line. A leaf whose filter error probe stays 0 in the injection epoch fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.

*Scope limit*: The test injects one corrupt value, both rails of the byte at 0. It grades nothing after the injection input falls, because the specification states no release of `lc_sigint_err_o`. It does not inject in an INVALID nibble, because the profile of INVALID is all zeros with and without the integrity logic. The profile of the 16 raw codes (legal and INVALID) belongs to leg A of `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`. The JTAG policy under the integrity error does not claim that the specification uses the same decoder in the filter as in the lifecycle controller: leg C injects at the decoder of the filter and does not use `lc_sigint_inject_i`. The open JTAG window tests keep the JTAG policy of the other states. The SEC_DIS revision enable is not set by the test (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).

'''''

[[sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 6.21 `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the leaf plan (the follow-on leg, the raw state code and the strap of the leg A read), and the preload hook stages the image of that plan from the seed.

*Source Module*: `sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor): `FEAT_CTRL`, `DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, the token registers. Approved probes and ports: `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, `lcc_demote_state_1_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_o`, the demotion lock probes, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o`, `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`, `lc_sigint_err_o`, `security_disable_o`, `secure_tm_req_i`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and the OTP image preload. A profile function written for this test from the Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile table and the disable-vector rules. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_ZERO` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. No inject. `otp_bank_probe_o` (the bank snapshot and diff of CHK-DEMOTE-OTP) and `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the post-guard statement of CHK-DEMOTE-OTP) are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Each leaf runs one leaf plan, which the seed draws in this order. First the follow-on leg, with the weights leg B 3, leg C 2, the RMA bypass of leg D 1, the earlier-owner cell of leg D 1, the walk of leg D 2 and leg E 2. Then the raw state code r that the leg allows: legs B and C draw r from the 16 raw codes with equal weight (the X bit of an RMA code is part of r); the RMA bypass draws RMA_SIP or RMA_CHIPLET with its X bit; the earlier-owner cell draws TEST_DEV or PROD; the walk draws TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END or RMA_SIP (the X bit drawn); leg E takes TEST_DEV. Then the strap s of the leg A read, 0 or 1 with equal weight. Then the free dimensions: the disable-vector pair; in leg B the reset vector v (1 to 15, with half weight for the vectors 2 and 10, which the cell walk of the leg already takes into a reset), the register A (1 or 2), the count L in 6 to 12 and the L operations, and the control bit b (a bit of the first `spare8` word that reads 0 in the image); in the earlier-owner cell the vector S; in the walk 16 of the 64 bit positions in a shuffled order, with double weight for the bits 0 to 4, 23 to 25, 47, 48 and 63, the owner of each other bit and the width of each write; in leg E the order in which the walk sets the five named bits. The bits 0 to 4, 23 to 25, 47, 48 and 63 take the owner `SIP_DIS` in TEST_DEV and `SYS_DIS` in PROD. A write takes one 64-bit access or two 32-bit accesses, drawn. Trap filters: the vector filter in every leaf except the walk, leg E and the earlier-owner cell; in each group `SIP_DIS` has a bit set and `SYS_DIS` has a bit set outside `SIP_DIS`, and in each debug group (DBG_1 and DBG_2) at least one bit lies outside both vectors; the earlier-owner cell stages `SIP_DIS` = S with a bit set in each group and `SYS_DIS` = 0; no `0x3` write to a demotion register; no write to bits [63:2] of a demotion register; the L random operations include at least one set operation. `reseed = 4` gives four leaf plans per regression, and every leaf grades one (raw code, strap) cell of leg A and one follow-on leg. These cells are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions. With 4 seeds the expected closure is about 25 regressions for the 16 raw codes of `x_raw16_sigint` (each INVALID code takes about 1 in 35 leaves), and about 30 for the 15 reset vectors of leg B; the case crosses of the two DFT ports close in about 6. The 32 combinations of leg E are not a coverage cell: at six combinations per leg E leaf they need about 50 regressions (`SEP_FCOV.adoc`, eFuse Bins not graded).

*Objective*: `FEAT_CTRL` equals the profile table row for every (raw state code, demotion, SEC_DIS) cell with a random disable-vector pair. All 16 raw codes give the profile of their class, with the X bit of the RMA codes part of the code, and the `LC_STATE` shadow word carries `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` in bits [7:0]. Demotion has no effect in PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and INVALID. SEC_DIS gives all ones in every state. RMA bypass leaves both disable shadows unchanged. The Function group ignores demotion. `secure_tm_req_i` is not a term of `FEAT_CTRL`. Each bit of `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` closes the feature bit of the same index through `~(SIP_DIS OR SYS_DIS)` where both apply. The reserved DBG_1, DBG_2 and Function bits follow their group formula. Runtime shadow writes only add bits. A later owner cannot clear an earlier restriction. `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` are independent write-one-to-set registers: `demote` sets only while `lock` is clear, `lock` sets with `demote` at 0 or 1, a zero write clears nothing, cold reset clears both, a write needs no authentication, each demotion state is a differentially coded 2-bit output, and demotion does not change the OTP state. In TEST_DEV with demotion 0/0, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` takes one value when Case 3 AND `SEP_FUSE_DBG` holds and the other value otherwise. `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o` takes one value when Case 2 AND `SMC_FUSE_DBG` holds and the other value otherwise. This holds over all 32 combinations of the five named disable bits, and no SMC-specific bit exists. The specification does not state the polarity of the two ports, so no check names a level (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, DFT fuse gate port polarity). Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Lifecycle States, Debug and Functional Feature Control, Disable Vector Format (SIP_DIS / SYS_DIS), DIS Vector Ownership: Predecessor-Programs-Disables, Combining Rule: OR then Invert, When Each DIS Vector Applies, Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS), Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile, In-Field Debug Demotion, Programming and Locking, Reset Behavior, Debug and Test Port Path Gating, Feature-Bit to Scan-Chain Mapping); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (registers `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc` (registers `FEAT_CTRL`, `DEMOTE_1`, `DEMOTE_2`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Fuse Fields, rows `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS`; RTL Integration); `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Implementation Requirements); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (ports `lcc_demote_state_1_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_o`, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o`, `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`).

*Test Coverage*: Leg A, raw code and profile, in every leaf: the raw value of the leaf image at the drawn strap value, with no demotion, so that over a merge of regressions each of the 16 raw values runs at both strap values, the X bit of the RMA codes included. INVALID is read after `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` is high, so INVALID after sense is separated from "sense did not run". With the strap at 1 the cells equal the strap-0 row. Leg B, demotion registers: a base state of each of the six classes, the four demotion cells (00, 10, 11, then 01 after a cold reset), the cold reset from each non-zero register vector (1 to 15), a random sequence of {set `demote`, set `lock`, write 0}, and a forced block in every leg B leaf (lock then demote on one register, demote then lock on the other, no reset between, a zero write after a set on both, a committed demote of 0 and of 1 each followed by a set and a zero write, and writes to the second register after the first is locked). Leg C, SEC_DIS: a `TOKEN_ZERO` match and a `TOKEN_OTHER` mismatch at each demotion cell of each state class. In RMA_CHIPLET the SEC_DIS cell is a control only. Leg D, bypass and owners: RMA bypass in RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET; the upper-bound gate on every read with SEC_DIS 0; a per-bit walk of 16 drawn bit positions in TEST_DEV, PROD (demoted), PROD_END or RMA_SIP, which covers all 64 bits of each state over a merge of regressions; the earlier-owner cell. Leg E, DFT fuse gate ports: a set-only walk in TEST_DEV with demotion 0/0 through six of the 32 combinations of {SEP_DBG 0, CHIPLET_DBG 1, SEP_FUSE_DBG 2, SMC_FUSE_DBG 3, SIP_DBG 24}, from all five open to all five closed. Every leg E leaf grades the gate formula on its drawn combinations. The test extends five open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_lcc_demote_feat_ctrl_matrix_test` walks TEST_DEV and PROD against the four demotion cells with a pinned disable pair and one PROD compose cell, locks one demotion group after its product walk and checks that a later demote write is ignored, and checks that the two DFT-inserted fuse-path disable ports follow `FEAT_CTRL` in its cells. `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` grades the OR-merge and the rejected clear of every fuse-field shadow word except `LC_STATE`, including `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS`. `sep_lcc_uvm_inbound_filter_gating_test` grades that `DEMOTE_1` opens DBG_1 and that `DEMOTE_2` alone opens DBG_2 and leaves DBG_1 closed. `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` and the `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_*` tests walk the lifecycle states with a pinned vector. This entry adds the 16 raw codes and the six states with random vector pairs, SEC_DIS at each demotion cell, RMA bypass, the per-bit owner rule over all 64 bits, the earlier-owner rule, the lock and write rules of both demotion registers in both orders with the zero write after a set, the cold reset from each register vector, the output pair encoding and the invariance of the OTP state, and the case conjunction of the five named disable bits at the two fuse DFT ports on drawn combinations. The `FEAT_CTRL` effect of a single demotion is a control of the register rules. The open owners keep the rest. Phase 1 groups `sep_lc_state_cg`, `sep_feat_ctrl_cg` and `sep_lc_demote_cg` take hits. Functional coverage: `sep_lc_feat_ctrl_profile_cg`, `sep_lc_feature_bit_semantics_cg`, `sep_lc_sigint_failclosed_cg` (`x_raw16_sigint`), `sep_lc_demotion_cg`, `sep_lc_demote_lock_cg`, `sep_dtp_gating_ladder_cg`, `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg` (`cp_match_phase` after sense, `x_state_demote_forced`, and the Match and Mismatch cells of `x_verdict_out`, graded by CHK-FEAT-CELL in leg C) and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg` (`x_state_strap_feat`). The cells of all groups come from the drawn leaf plan, so they are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions (Randomization).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the leaf plan and the free dimensions (Randomization). Print `SEED seed=<n> leg=<leg> raw=0x<r> stm=<s>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Run leg A and then only the steps of the follow-on leg. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. Every read of `FEAT_CTRL` is a 64-bit frontdoor read, with a sample of `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` at the same time. `secure_tm_req_i` is the drawn strap s in leg A and 0 in every follow-on leg, because the specification blocks a shadow write and a fuse program while secure test mode is active (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection).
. Leg A, every leaf. Stage the image of the leaf plan: `{~r, r}` for the drawn raw value r, the vector pair of the follow-on leg (a filter-legal pair, or the exception that the leg states) and no lock bit. This is the only t=0 image of the leaf, and the follow-on leg uses it. Hold the drawn strap s from before reset release. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Leg A. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Sample `lc_sigint_err_o` and the `LC_STATE` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the profile row of the raw class, `lc_sigint_err_o` at 0 and the shadow word `{~r, r}`. When s = 1, lower the strap while a cold reset is asserted, release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`; the follow-on leg starts from that sense, with the strap at 0.
. Leg B. The image holds the drawn raw code, a filter-legal pair and no lock bit. Take the OTP bank snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Record as the undemoted reference: both output pairs, the `LC_STATE` shadow word and the profile row.
. Leg B. Define the check cycle. After an operation, read `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2` (bits [63:2] read 0), read `FEAT_CTRL`, sample both output pairs and both demotion lock probes, sample the `LC_STATE` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, take the OTP bank diff against the snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`, and record the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Compare each with the golden.
. Leg B, cell walk. Run a check cycle (cell 00). Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1`; run a check cycle (cell 10). Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2`; run a check cycle (cell 11). Log the register vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, cell walk. Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2` only; run a check cycle (cell 01). Log the register vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, reset vector. The register vector is (demote 1 as bit 3, lock 1, demote 2, lock 2 as bit 0) = v. From the clean state, write only the bits of v: for each register, in the order register 1, register 2, write `0x1` first when its demote bit is in v and then `0x2` when its lock bit is in v. Run a check cycle after each write. The path never overshoots, because it writes no bit outside v and writes demote before lock. Log the vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, random sequence. Draw the L operations from {set `demote` (write `0x1`), set `lock` (write `0x2`), write 0} on a drawn register. Run a check cycle after each. Log the vector. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, forced block. Take the drawn register A and the other register B. On A: set `lock`, then set `demote`, then write 0, then set `lock` again. On B, after A is locked: set `demote`, write 0, set `lock`, then set `demote` and write 0. Run a check cycle after each write. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write to `DEMOTE_1` or `DEMOTE_2`. Kind: a `demote` write after `lock`, and a zero write after a set. Expected response: no change of the register. Log the vector (non-zero: both locks are set). Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Run a check cycle (clean state).
. Leg B, control program. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1 and `efuse_addr` = the bit address of a drawn bit b of the first word of `spare8` that reads 0 in the staged image (8 times the byte offset `0x3E0` plus b; the field is unlocked, and its word is not all ones, so such a bit exists). Write the register again with `efuse_program_go` = 1 in the same write as the other fields. Poll `program_done` until it reads 1. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the first `spare8` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`: bit b reads 1, and the `LC_STATE` shadow word equals the undemoted reference. Take the OTP bank diff against the snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`; it shows bit b and no other bit. Record the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the go write to the `program_done` read; one program command with the address of bit b shows.
. Leg C. Run `CHK-BIND` first. The image holds the drawn raw code and a filter-legal pair.
. Leg C. Reach demotion cell 00 (no write). Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the profile row with SEC_DIS at 0.
. Leg C, SEC_DIS cell. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16. Wait until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. On the first match of the leaf, `security_disable_o` must rise, else fail the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect all ones.
. Leg C, SEC_DIS cell. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I` and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 16. Wait until `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`. The code before this go is the `0x15` of the previous step, so a go that has no effect times out the wait. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the profile row of the cell (SEC_DIS 0) and `security_disable_o` low.
. Leg C. Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1` (cell 10). Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Run the SEC_DIS cell of steps 14 and 15. Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2` (cell 11). Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Run the SEC_DIS cell again.
. Leg C. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_2` only (cell 01). Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Run the SEC_DIS cell again. Cold reset.
. Leg D, RMA bypass. The image holds RMA_SIP or RMA_CHIPLET, with its X bit, and a filter-legal pair. Read `SIP_DIS` (`0x1093_0018`) and `SYS_DIS` (`0x1093_0020`) as 64-bit reads. Read `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, earlier owner. The image holds TEST_DEV or PROD with `SIP_DIS` = S, a vector with a bit set in each group, and `SYS_DIS` = 0 (an exception to the vector filter: the leaf writes `SYS_DIS`). In PROD, write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1` and to `DEMOTE_2`. Read `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, earlier owner. Write the bitwise complement of S to `SYS_DIS`. Write 0 to `SIP_DIS`. Error injection. Point: the frontdoor write of 0 to `SIP_DIS`. Kind: a clear write to a write-one-to-set register. Expected response: no change. Read `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, walk. The image holds TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END or RMA_SIP with `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` both 0 (an exception to the vector filter: the walk builds the vectors by writes), with no lock bit. In PROD, write `0x1` to `DEMOTE_1` and to `DEMOTE_2`. Read `FEAT_CTRL` (the start row).
. Leg D, walk. For each of the 16 drawn positions b, in the shuffled order: take the owner (pinned or drawn) and the write width. Write `1 << b` to the owner shadow, as one 64-bit write or as two 32-bit writes (the word without the bit gets 0). Read the owner shadow and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg D, walk. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg E. The image holds TEST_DEV, `SIP_DIS` = 0 and `SYS_DIS` = 0 (an exception to the vector filter: the walk builds the vector by writes) and no lock bit. Control cell 1 (all five named bits 0): read `FEAT_CTRL` and sample `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` and `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o`. Then set the five named bits one at a time in the drawn order: write `1 << b` to `SIP_DIS` (a set-only shadow write, legal because the leaf runs in TEST_DEV with every lock bit 0 and the strap at 0), read `SIP_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`, and sample both ports. The last write gives control cell 2 (all five named bits 1). The walk visits six of the 32 combinations, one for each count of set bits, with no further cold reset.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|Per the common rules of this bucket: the SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the digest that the bench binds as the SEC_DIS expected digest (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A leg C leaf runs it before the first SEC_DIS go. Anchor: the bound constant at the bench against a SHA-256 of the 32 zero bytes. Control: none; the check grades the bench. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h>`.
|CHK-FEAT-CELL
|`FEAT_CTRL` equals the 64-bit profile row for each (raw code, demotion, SEC_DIS, strap) cell, and the `LC_STATE` shadow word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` equals `{~raw, raw}` with `lc_sigint_err_o` low (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile, Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS); `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`). A DUT that decodes a raw value to the wrong class, ignores the X bit, applies demotion in INVALID, forgets `SYS_DIS` in TEST_DEV, lets the strap open a bit or applies the wrong group formula fails. In leg C the SEC_DIS 1 cell is all ones with `security_disable_o` high, and the cell after the mismatch is the row of the demotion cell with `security_disable_o` low. Anchor: the `FEAT_CTRL` register readback and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` (both equal the golden), and the shadow probe. Control: the undemoted cell of the same image shows the closed bits that the demoted or SEC_DIS cell opens; in RMA_CHIPLET the SEC_DIS cell is all ones in both rows and is logged as a control with `ctl=1`. Evidence: `CHK-FEAT-CELL PASS seed=<n> leg=<a/c> raw=0x<r> state=<name> dem=<d1d2> secdis=<0/1> stm=<0/1> sip=0x<64b> sys=0x<64b> exp=0x<64b> got=0x<64b> lc_shadow=0x<b> sigint=0 row=<profile row> sdis_port=<0/1/na> ctl=<0/1>`.
|CHK-RMA-BYPASS
|In RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET both disable shadows read the staged values and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the described row: `~SIP_DIS` per group in RMA_SIP and all ones in RMA_CHIPLET (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections DIS Vector Ownership: Predecessor-Programs-Disables, When Each DIS Vector Applies). A DUT that erases a disable shadow on RMA entry fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` and of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the vector filter makes the staged pair non-zero in each group, so an erased shadow differs from the staged value, and in RMA_SIP `~SIP_DIS` differs from `~(SIP_DIS OR SYS_DIS)`. Evidence: `CHK-RMA-BYPASS PASS seed=<n> state=<name> shadow_sip=0x<64b> shadow_sys=0x<64b> staged_equal=1 feat=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-GATE-BOUND
|On every read of `FEAT_CTRL` with SEC_DIS 0 and a mask that is not all ones, no bit of `FEAT_CTRL` lies outside the upper-bound mask of the state. The mask is the OR of the SEC_DIS = 0 rows of that state in the profile table, built with the model shadow values (the staged image and the OR-merge of the writes of the leaf), not with a shadow read-back. The SEC_DIS = 1 rows are all ones and are not part of the mask (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile). A DUT that opens a bit that no row opens fails. Anchor: the `FEAT_CTRL` register readback against the mask from the model shadow values; the shadow readback is graded against the same model in CHK-DIS-STEP and CHK-RMA-BYPASS. A read whose mask is all ones cannot show a stray bit and is not graded: it logs `OBS-GATE-BOUND LOG seed=<n> state=<name> mask=all_ones graded=0`. That holds for every read in RMA_CHIPLET and for a read in TEST_DEV or RMA_SIP with a zero vector (the start row of the walk) or with only the five named bits set (leg E). Control: for a filter-legal pair the mask is not all ones in TEST_DEV, PROD, PROD_END, RMA_SIP and INVALID, so a stray bit shows in the legs that stage such a pair. Evidence: `CHK-GATE-BOUND PASS seed=<n> state=<name> mask=0x<64b> feat=0x<64b> extra_bits=0x0`.
|CHK-DIS-STEP
|After each walk write the owner shadow equals the OR-merge golden and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the row for the cumulative vectors. In RMA_SIP a `SYS_DIS` bit changes the shadow and not `FEAT_CTRL`.
