After the cold reset that ends the walk, both shadows read 0 (the staged image) and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the undemoted row of that image (`sep_efuse_map.adoc`, registers `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS`: write-one-to-set; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Combining Rule: OR then Invert, Reset Behavior). A DUT that clears a bit, overwrites the register, ignores a reserved group bit or keeps a write over reset fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of the owner shadow and of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the bits 0 to 4, 23 to 25, 47, 48 and 63 take a pinned owner, and over a merge of regressions the drawn positions cover all 64 bits of each state; the start row has the bit open, so each write shows as a closed bit (where the profile exposes it). Evidence: `CHK-DIS-STEP PASS seed=<n> state=<name> step=<k> bit=<b> owner=<SIP/SYS> wr=0x<64b> shadow_exp=0x<64b> shadow_got=0x<64b> feat_exp=0x<64b> feat_got=0x<64b>`; `CHK-DIS-STEP PASS seed=<n> state=<name> step=reset sip=0x0 sys=0x0 feat_exp=0x<64b> feat_got=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-DIS-EARLIER
|A later owner cannot clear an earlier restriction: `SIP_DIS` reads S after the write of 0 and the write of the complement of S to `SYS_DIS`, and `FEAT_CTRL` AND S is 0 (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Combining Rule: OR then Invert). The leaf fails if S is 0. A DUT that lets the write of 0 clear `SIP_DIS` shows `FEAT_CTRL` = S and fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS` and `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: S has a bit set in each group by the staging rule of the cell, and the complement written to `SYS_DIS` closes every bit outside S, so `FEAT_CTRL` can be non-zero only inside S. Evidence: `CHK-DIS-EARLIER PASS seed=<n> state=<name> S=0x<64b> sip_got=0x<64b> sys=0x<64b> feat=0x<64b> feat_and_S=0x0`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-STEP
|After each operation both registers (frontdoor readback, bits [63:2] at 0) equal the two-bit golden: `demote` sets only if `lock` = 0, `lock` sets always, zero changes nothing (`sep_lifecycle_ctrl.adoc`, registers `DEMOTE_1` and `DEMOTE_2`, access RW1S; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Programming and Locking). A DUT that lets `demote` change after `lock`, clears on a zero write or couples the two registers fails. `FEAT_CTRL` equals the profile row for the cumulative demotion. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of both registers and of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: in every sequence the second register sets `demote` while its lock is clear and after the first register is locked. In TEST_DEV and PROD the `FEAT_CTRL` after a committed demote differs from the undemoted one, because the vector filter leaves a bit closed in each group and a bit open in each debug group. In PROD_END, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and INVALID demotion has no effect, so `FEAT_CTRL` equals the undemoted row after every operation. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-STEP PASS seed=<n> step=<k> reg=<1/2> op=<set_demote/set_lock/zero> reg_exp=0x<v> reg_got=0x<v> feat_exp=0x<64b> feat_got=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-ENC
|Each output pair (port probe) is complementary, the pair of a demoted register differs from the pair of an undemoted one, and each lock bit probe equals the lock bit of the register readback (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section RTL Integration: the lifecycle controller differentially encodes its two demotion outputs; `port_table.adoc`, ports `lcc_demote_state_1_o`, `lcc_demote_state_2_o`). The test names no level of a pair. A DUT with a non-complementary pair, a pair that does not change on a demotion, or a lock probe that differs from the register fails. Anchor: the port probes and the register readback. Control: the undemoted reference pair is recorded after sense, so the demoted pair is compared with a pair that the same leaf sampled. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-ENC PASS seed=<n> reg=<1/2> undemoted=0b<xx> demoted=0b<xx> complementary=1 distinct=1 lock_probe=<0/1> lock_reg=<0/1>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-OTP
|The `LC_STATE` shadow word equals the staged byte `{~raw, raw}` of the image (the undemoted reference) after every operation and after every cold-reset sense of the leg, because demotion does not change the OTP state (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Lifecycle States). The bank keeps every program over reset, and a program reaches the shadow at the next sense (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection), so a demotion that burns an `LC_STATE` fuse shows at the next sense. A DUT that burns an `LC_STATE` fuse or changes the shadow word on a demotion fails. Anchor: the `LC_STATE` word at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Control: one frontdoor program of a `spare8` bit that reads 0 in the staged image, at the end of leg B, shows as 1 in the `spare8` shadow word after the next sense, so a burn can show at a resense and the control cannot pass without the burn. From the first check cycle of leg B to the end of the forced block, the check grades that no bank bit changes (the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` is none) and that no program command reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`); the control program is outside this window. Control on the same taps: the control program of bit b shows one program command with the address of bit b at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`, and the bank diff after it shows bit b and no other bit; a missing command or a missing bit fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-OTP PASS seed=<n> lc_shadow=0x<v> ref=0x<v> lc_shadow_after_sense=0x<v> ctl_bit=<b> ctl_spare8_staged=0x<w> ctl_spare8=0x<w> ctl_cmd=1 ctl_diff=<a>`.
|CHK-DEMOTE-RESET
|After a cold reset both registers read 0, and `FEAT_CTRL` and both pairs are at the undemoted reference (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections In-Field Debug Demotion, Reset Behavior). A DUT that keeps state over reset fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of both registers, `FEAT_CTRL` and the port probes after `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Control: the vector before the reset is non-zero and logged, and the reset follows a state that differs from the clean state. Evidence: `CHK-DEMOTE-RESET PASS seed=<n> stage=<cell/vec/rand/forced> vec_before=0x<v> regs=0x0 feat=0x<64b> pairs_ref=1`.
|CHK-DTP-FEAT
|In leg E, `FEAT_CTRL` bit k is the inverse of disable bit k for each of the five named bits (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Disable Vector Format (SIP_DIS / SYS_DIS), Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile: TEST_DEV with demotion 0/0 gives `~(SIP_DIS OR SYS_DIS)`). A DUT that maps a bit to the wrong index or drops the inversion fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `FEAT_CTRL`. Control: the staged `SYS_DIS` is 0, so each named bit closes its feature bit through `SIP_DIS` alone, and the walk starts with all five bits open and ends with all five closed, so each named bit is read at both values in every leg E leaf. Evidence: `CHK-DTP-FEAT PASS seed=<n> bits=0b<SEP,CHP,SEPF,SMCF,SIP> feat_bits=0b<xxxxx> feat=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-DTP-PORT
|In leg E, `sep_fuse_dft_disable_o` has one value on every cell where SIP_DBG, CHIPLET_DBG, SEP_DBG and SEP_FUSE_DBG are all open and the other value on every other cell. `smc_fuse_dft_disable_o` has one value on every cell where SIP_DBG, CHIPLET_DBG and SMC_FUSE_DBG are all open and the other value on every other cell (`lifecycle_controller.adoc`, section Debug and Test Port Path Gating, rows DFT-inserted SEP fuse path and DFT-inserted SMC fuse path; `port_table.adoc`). No check names a level or compares a port with a reference taken from the DUT: the cells compare with each other by case class. A DUT that drops a term, adds an SMC bit or ties the port constant fails. Anchor: the two port values per cell, compared with the port values of the two control cells of the same leaf. Control: the all-open cell and the all-closed cell of the same leaf show different values on each port, else the leaf fails; so both classes of each port are non-empty in every leg E leaf. Evidence: `CHK-DTP-PORT PASS seed=<n> bits=0b<SEP,CHP,SEPF,SMCF,SIP> sep_port=<0/1> sep_class=<open_case/other> smc_port=<0/1> smc_class=<open_case/other> ctl_open=<0/1,0/1> ctl_closed=<0/1,0/1>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test grades no write of `0x3` to a demotion register and no write to bits [63:2] of one, because the specification states no effect for them (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). It names no level of the two DFT fuse gate ports and no polarity of the demotion output pair. It runs the SEC_DIS cell only at strap 0. It sets no SEC_DIS revision enable and presents no token with the strap at 1 (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The shadow write and program legs hold the strap at 0, and the effect of secure test mode on a write belongs to `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test`. Leg E does not grade demotion, owner veto or RMA bypass. The meaning of a debug-disable bit at the DTP boundary is not graded. The open tests named in Test Coverage keep the cells that they grade.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test]]
===== 6.22 `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the enable and the start state of that image (the preload hook stages it from the seed). Every leaf runs leg A. A leaf whose image is TEST_DEV with `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 0 runs leg B after leg A on the same image.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip as the CPU-side master of target `lsu_stub_all_live` (the frontdoor): the token registers, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `FEAT_CTRL`. Approved: `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, the OTP image preload and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface (`sep_efuse_direct_read_seq`; the read interface is not under test here). A SHA-256 model for the digest staging. A profile function written for this test from the Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile table. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. No inject. `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the post-guard statements of CHK-TRANSIENT-NOBURN and CHK-PROG-LC) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The draws are: the pair (enable, start state), where the pair (0, TEST_DEV) takes a weight of 1 in 3, because only that image also runs leg B, and the other nine pairs of enable {0, 1} and start state {TEST_DEV, PROD, RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, INVALID (raw `0101`)} share the other 2 in 3 with equal weight; the token script, one of {SIP only, CHIPLET only, CHIPLET then SIP, SIP then CHIPLET} with equal weight; the X bit of an RMA start state; the disable-vector pair; and the free spare bit of the window control. Trap filters: one go bit per write; no program before sense done; the vector filter. `reseed = 4` gives four images per regression, and a regression runs leg B with a probability of about 0.8 (1 - (2/3)^4). The (enable, channel, start state) cells of leg A are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions, about 16 regressions for the 20 cells of `x_transient`.

*Objective*: With `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 1 a token match updates the `LC_STATE` shadow without a bus write. The chiplet path needs RMA_SIP set, and a CHIPLET match without it changes nothing. With `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 0 a match leaves `LC_STATE` unchanged and the result code still reads Match. A cold reset restores the sensed state. The update burns no fuse. A program of `LC_STATE` bit 1 lands in the OTP bank only when the RMA_SIP token has a match, and a program of bit 2 lands only when the RMA_CHIPLET token has a match. Bit 1 is set before every bit 2 cell of this leaf. A program with only the other channel matched changes no bank bit. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization; Fuse Fields, rows `LC_STATE` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Submission, Post Token Matching Handling); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (register `LC_STATE`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (register `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (section Lifecycle States, row TEST_DEV, which places the token-digest programming). The specification does not say whether a retained match acts again after a cold reset with the enable at 1, or whether a blocked CHIPLET match acts after a later SIP match sets bit 1. So every match in this leaf is replaced by a mismatch go on the same channel before the next cold reset and before a later SIP step.

*Test Coverage*: Leg A, transient update: `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0 and 1, staged through the OTP image (the register has access R); five start states; four token scripts (`TOKEN_T1` on SIP, `TOKEN_T2` on CHIPLET); a CHIPLET match with no RMA_SIP state; the result code with the enable at 0; the reset restore; the absence of a fuse burn; a window control in the same leaf (a program of a free spare bit that lands inside the window W). Leg B, program authorization: a TEST_DEV image with the enable at 0; a program of `LC_STATE` bit 1 with only the CHIPLET token matched and then with the SIP token matched; a program of bit 2 with only the SIP token matched and then with the CHIPLET token matched. The test extends three open tests, and only the new cells are graded. `sep_lcc_lc_state_w1s_transient_test` walks the transient update from one start state, and `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` walks the SIP and CHIPLET match from PROD and grades the refusal of an `LC_STATE` program with a wrong token. `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test` grades the write-locked refusal of a program. This entry adds the enable 0 and 1 matrix, five start states, four token scripts, a CHIPLET match with no RMA_SIP state, the result code with the enable at 0, the reset restore, the absence of a fuse burn, and the program of `LC_STATE` bits 1 and 2 with only the other channel matched against the program with the matching channel. The program of bit 2 with only the SIP channel matched adds only the held SIP Match read to the refusal that `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` grades with a mismatched CHIPLET token, so it runs as a control with `role=control`. The program of bit 1 with only the CHIPLET channel matched is a new cell. The open owners keep the rest. Phase 1 group `sep_lc_transition_cg` takes hits. Functional coverage: `sep_lc_illegal_transition_cg` (seed-dependent: `x_transient`, one drawn (enable, start state) pair and script per leaf, closed over a merge of regressions) and `sep_efuse_lock_policy_cg` (`x_lc_prog_gate`, except the cell of bit 2 with only the other channel matched, which is a control here).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the values of the Randomization field from the seed. Print `SEED seed=<n> en=<0/1> start=<name> script=<s> leg_b=<0/1>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. A cold reset in this leaf follows a mismatch go on every channel that holds a match.
. Digest staging. Define the digest staging that every stage step of this leaf uses. Stage the RMA digests by the digest rule of this bucket: `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T1` and `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T2`. The packing is a stimulus choice, and no check grades it. The first Match read of each channel in the leaf is the control of that staging. A first Match read that shows another code fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and the later steps are not graded.
. Legs A and B. Stage the image of the leaf, which is its only t=0 image: the drawn enable and start state (the X bit drawn for an RMA state), the digests and a filter-legal pair. Leg B runs only when this image is TEST_DEV with `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` = 0, and it starts after the cold reset and sense of step 9; a leaf with another image ends after step 9. Preload. Cold reset. Measure the sense time from reset release to `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. The idle window W is a multiple of that time (the multiple is a test parameter). Every "never" wait below lasts W. Define the program wait. After the go write of a program, wait the full W with no early exit, because nothing shows that `program_busy` has risen before the first poll. Then read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and require `program_busy` 0. For a program that must land, require `program_done` 1 and read the OTP word of the programmed bit through the direct read; the bit reads 1. For a program that must be refused, log `program_busy` and `program_done`, and read the same OTP word through the direct read; the bit reads 0. A timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Legs A and B. Window control. Program a free spare bit: write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1 and `efuse_addr` = the bit address of the drawn spare bit (8 times the byte offset of its field plus the bit index), then write it again with `efuse_program_go` = 1. Run the program wait for a program that must land. The bit reads 1 at the direct read inside W. A leaf in which it does not fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, because a wait of W could not show a program that lands. Record the program commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the go write to the direct read; one program command with the address of the spare bit shows, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Log `OBS-WINDOW-CTL LOG seed=<n> w_cycles=<w> landed=1`.
. Leg A. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg A. Run the script step by step. For a step on channel c: write the eight words of the channel token (`TOKEN_T1` for SIP, `TOKEN_T2` for CHIPLET) to its array and write 1 to its `TOKEN_EOP` bit (SIP bit 0, CHIPLET bit 8). Wait until the match register of c reads `0x15`. Wait the window W. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word and `FEAT_CTRL`. Error injection, for a CHIPLET step with `LC_STATE` bit 1 clear. Point: the `TOKEN_EOP` go with a matching CHIPLET token. Kind: a transition request without its precondition. Expected response: no change of the `LC_STATE` shadow.
. Leg A. After a CHIPLET step and before a later SIP step, write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the CHIPLET array and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 8. Wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` (`0x1093_0568`) reads `0x2a`.
. Leg A. After the script, for each channel whose match register reads `0x15` (a held Match), write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to its array and write 1 to its `TOKEN_EOP` bit. Wait until its match register reads `0x2a`; the `0x15` read before the go makes a go that has no effect time out the wait. A channel whose Match step 7 already ended takes no go here. Read the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP through the direct read; expect the staged word (the raw nibble of the start state).
. Leg A. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word and `FEAT_CTRL`.
. Leg B, bit 1 cells. Present `TOKEN_T2` on the CHIPLET channel and wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`; the SIP channel holds no match. Program `LC_STATE` bit 1: write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1 and `efuse_addr` = the bit address of bit 1 (8 times `0xC`, plus 1), then write it again with `efuse_program_go` = 1. Error injection. Point: the `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go for `LC_STATE` bit 1 with only the CHIPLET channel matched. Kind: a token-gated program without its token. Expected response: no change in the bank. The status value is not stated and is not graded. Wait the full W. Read the status and log `program_busy`. Read the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP through the direct read; expect the staged raw nibble with bit 1 clear. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word.
. Leg B, bit 1 cells. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the CHIPLET array and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 8. Wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`. No CHIPLET match is held when the SIP match follows.
. Leg B, bit 1 cells. Present `TOKEN_T1` on the SIP channel and wait until `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. Repeat the program of bit 1 and run the program wait for a program that must land; the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP reads with bit 1 set. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the value before the program.
. Leg B. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SIP array and write 1 to `TOKEN_EOP` bit 0. Wait until `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x2a`; `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` already reads `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the byte `{~r, r}` with r the raw nibble before the program OR bit 1 (`0010`, RMA_SIP).
. Leg B, bit 2 cells. Present `TOKEN_T1` on the SIP channel and wait until `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. Read `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` and expect a code other than `0x15`: the CHIPLET mismatch of step 11 is the last CHIPLET go, so the channel holds no Match. Log the code. No CHIPLET go is written here, because a mismatch go from a held Mismatch cannot show that it ran. Program `LC_STATE` bit 2 (bit address 8 times `0xC`, plus 2). Error injection. Point: the `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go for `LC_STATE` bit 2 with only the SIP channel matched. Kind: a token-gated program without its token. Expected response: no change in the bank. The status value is not stated and is not graded. Wait the full W. Read the status and log `program_busy`. Read the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP through the direct read; expect raw `0010`, with bit 2 clear. This cell is the control of the open refusal and logs `role=control`.
. Leg B, bit 2 cells. Present `TOKEN_T2` on the CHIPLET channel and wait until `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH` reads `0x15`. Repeat the program of bit 2 and run the program wait for a program that must land; the `LC_STATE` word of the OTP reads with bit 2 set. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the value before the program.
. Leg B. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SIP and CHIPLET arrays and write the two go bits in two writes. Wait until both match registers read `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Read the `LC_STATE` shadow word; expect the byte `{~r, r}` with r = `0110` (RMA_CHIPLET).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-TRANSIENT
|After each step the `LC_STATE` shadow (frontdoor readback), `FEAT_CTRL` and the match code equal the golden (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields, rows `LC_STATE` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN`; `token_processing.adoc`, section Post Token Matching Handling). With the enable at 1, a SIP match sets bit 1 from TEST_DEV and PROD, a CHIPLET match sets bit 2 when bit 1 is set, a CHIPLET match with bit 1 clear changes nothing, and INVALID stays INVALID. With the enable at 0 the shadow never changes. The code is `0x15` for each match in both cases. A DUT that updates with the enable at 0 or skips the RMA_SIP precondition fails. Anchor: the `LC_STATE` word readback, `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. The golden raw state comes from the rules above, and `FEAT_CTRL` from the profile function for the new state and the staged vectors. Control: the window control shows that a program started at its go lands inside W, and every cell with an update moves the state inside the same W, so a "never" wait of W is as long as a landing wait. Evidence: `CHK-TRANSIENT PASS seed=<n> en=<0/1> start=<name> step=<k> tok=<sip/chp> code=0x15 exp_byte=0x<b> got_byte=0x<b> exp_feat=0x<64b> got_feat=0x<64b>`; the window control logs `OBS-WINDOW-CTL`.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-NOBURN
|The `LC_STATE` word of the OTP, read through the direct read after the scripts of leg A, equals the staged word: a match authorizes a change and burns no fuse (`token_processing.adoc`, section Post Token Matching Handling). A DUT that burns an `LC_STATE` bit on a transient update fails. Anchor: the direct read of the `LC_STATE` OTP word. Control: the free spare bit of the window control reads 1 at the same direct read, so the read can show a burn. The check also grades that no program command reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the first go of the leg A script (step 6) to the direct read of step 8; the window-control program of step 4 and the programs of leg B are outside this window. Control on the same tap: the window-control program of step 4 shows one program command with the address of the spare bit at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; a leaf in which it does not fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-TRANSIENT-NOBURN PASS seed=<n> lc_otp_word=0x<w> staged_word=0x<w> ctl_spare_bit=1 ctl_cmd=1`.
|CHK-TRANSIENT-RESET
|After the final cold reset and sense done the shadow equals the staged byte and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the staged profile (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing and the opening paragraph: the sensing FSM copies each fuse field into its shadow register after cold reset). A DUT that keeps the update over reset fails. The mismatch go before the reset reads `0x2a`, so no retained match is left. Anchor: the `LC_STATE` word readback and the `FEAT_CTRL` readback after the sense. Control: the shadow before the reset differs from the staged byte in every cell that updated, and the pre-reset codes are `0x2a` on every channel that took a match. Evidence: `CHK-TRANSIENT-RESET PASS seed=<n> pre_reset_codes=0x2a staged_byte=0x<b> got_byte=0x<b> exp_feat=0x<64b> got_feat=0x<64b>`.
|CHK-PROG-LC
|A program of `LC_STATE` bit 1 or bit 2 lands in the OTP only when the channel that gates the bit is matched, and changes no bank bit when only the other channel is matched (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization: only on a match can `LC_STATE[1]` and `LC_STATE[2]` be programmed; section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`: setting bit 2 needs bit 1 set before the write). The shadow word equals its value before the program until the resense. After the resense its byte is `{~r, r}`, with r the raw nibble before the program OR the programmed bit (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection: a program reaches the shadow at the next sense; section Fuse Fields, row `LC_STATE`: the shadow byte is `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`). A DUT that accepts the other channel's match fails. Anchor: the direct read of the `LC_STATE` OTP word and the `LC_STATE` shadow readback. Every refused-program wait lasts the full W, and the direct read follows it. Control: the program with the matching channel lands in the same leaf, within the same W, for bit 1 and for bit 2, and the window control shows that a program started at its go lands inside W. So a wrongly accepted program that starts late would land inside the refused wait and show at the direct read. The bit-1 cell with only the CHIPLET token matched is new. The bit-2 cell with only the SIP token matched adds only the held SIP Match read to the refusal that `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` grades, so it logs `role=control`. The check also grades that no program command reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the go of a refused program to the end of its wait W. Control on the same tap: the program with the matching channel shows one program command with the address of the programmed bit at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within its wait W, for bit 1 and for bit 2; a missing command fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-LC PASS seed=<n> bit=<1/2> match=<other_only/own> role=<new/control> shim_cmd=<0/1> w_cycles=<w> lc_otp_word=0x<w> landed=<0/1> shadow_pre=0x<b> shadow_after_sense=0x<b/na> exp_after_sense=0x<b/na> state_after_sense=<name/na>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not grade the status value of a refused program, the effect of a retained match after a cold reset with the enable at 1, or the effect of a blocked CHIPLET match after a later SIP match sets bit 1 (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); every match is replaced by a mismatch go before those events. It does not grade the digest packing. The write-locked refusal of a program belongs to `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`. A token match fault and a SEC_DIS go are not part of this test. The open tests named in Test Coverage keep the cells that they grade.
[[sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test]]
===== 6.23 `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 3 seeds; every leaf walks all 18 legal mismatch cells and the independence cell on each of the three channels; tool: VCS, because the no-reset check needs X on unreset flops; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor: the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live` (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`), with the response code of every access (approved). The OTP bank model with its image preload (approved). Ports `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and `security_disable_o` (approved). `secure_tm_req_i` stays at 0. The test computes the SHA-256 golden of each token. The tap of the 24 token word registers (CHK-NORESET) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: mismatch shape (7: flip, swap, byte swap, zeros, ones, digest as token, foreign token) and channel (3: RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, SEC_DIS); every leaf walks the 18 legal (shape, channel) cells in an order that the seed draws; on SEC_DIS only flip, ones, digest as token and foreign token are legal. Also drawn: the order of the 18 cells, the order of the three channels in the independence cell, the word-write order of every array write, the decoy subset, the flip position (0 to 255), and the words of the swap and of the byte swap. Trap filters: no `TOKEN_ZERO` on the SEC_DIS channel outside its match steps; a swap of two distinct words; a byte swap of a non-palindromic word; no go on an array with no written word; no word write between a go and its non-Idle result; one go bit per write. `reseed = 3` samples other cell orders, word orders, decoy subsets, flip positions and swap or byte-swap words. The cell set is the same on every seed.

*Objective*: Each of three channels hashes its eight-word array (index 7 first, no byte swap) on its own go bit and shows Idle `0x00`, Match `0x15` or Mismatch `0x2a`, and no other code. A go clears the held result until the new result (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: a start clears digest validity, so the comparison returns idle while the new digest is unavailable). The specification gives no cycle count for that Idle interval, so the leaf grades the Idle observation relative to the hash time that it measures. Writing the token words of a channel alone does not change its result and does not start a comparison. The RMA channels compare to their own OTP digest and read Idle until sense completes. The SEC_DIS channel compares to a constant that does not depend on OTP. A digest used as a token never matches. The token arrays have no reset. After a cold reset a retained digest gives its result again once the comparison prerequisites hold. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission); `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Fuse Sensing and the Shadow Map, Reset Persistence, Implementation Requirements); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (section eFuse Token Registers Address Map; registers `TOKEN_EOP`, `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH`). The specification does not say what happens when one `TOKEN_EOP` write sets several go bits, so no step writes two go bits (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).

*Test Coverage*: The unreset token arrays. A go before sense done on RMA_SIP and on RMA_CHIPLET. Word-write order and decoy writes before the final words, on each channel. Result hold under word writes, on each channel. The Idle read after a go while a result is held, on each channel. The 18 legal (shape, channel) mismatch cells: the seven shapes on each RMA channel and four shapes on SEC_DIS. Independence of the three channels, with the SEC_DIS channel given an RMA token. Retention of a digest over cold reset, with sensing held off by a retained SEC_DIS match and with sensing running. The test extends `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test` (match and mismatch of the SIP and CHIPLET tokens), `sep_sec_dis_override_test` (the SEC_DIS match) and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test` (the digest latch). This test grades only the new cells named above. The three open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_token_channel_cg` (every coverpoint; bins `0x00`, `0x15` and `0x2a` of `cp_code`), `sep_efuse_map_geometry_cg` (the `TOKEN_EOP` write cell and the three match read cells of `x_token_blk_op`) and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg` (`x_digest_replay`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the order of the 18 (shape, channel) cells, the order of the three channels of the independence cell, the word orders, the decoy subsets and the flip positions from the seed. In the steps below, "write token X to c" writes the eight words of X to the array of channel c in a drawn order, word k holding bits [32k+31:32k] of X. "Go on c" writes 1 to the go bit of c in `TOKEN_EOP` (bit 0 RMA_SIP, bit 8 RMA_CHIPLET, bit 16 SEC_DIS) with no other bit set. "Poll c" reads the match register of c until it reads a value other than `0x00`. A "read run on c" reads the match register of c back to back from the go until two consecutive reads return the same code other than `0x00`. It counts the reads as `W_hash` of the channel and the reads that return `0x00` as `n_idle`. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. Every go on SEC_DIS runs with `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Stage the OTP image: TEST_DEV; the SHA-256 digest of `TOKEN_T1` in `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` and that of `TOKEN_T2` in `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` (word k holding bits [32k+31:32k]); every other 256-bit field at its fixed vector; `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0, so that no match updates the state shadow; a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits.
. Run `CHK-BIND`. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. No-reset cell. Before any token write of the leaf, read the tap of the 24 token word registers. Expect X on every word.
. Walk. Run the sub-steps below once per channel, in a drawn order. The token T is `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_SIP, `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_CHIPLET and `TOKEN_ZERO` on SEC_DIS. The foreign token F is `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_SIP and `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_CHIPLET and on SEC_DIS.
.. Write wrong decoy words to a drawn subset of the array words, then write token T to the channel (the decoys are overwritten). Read the token word tap at the first word written, as the control of the no-reset cell.
.. Go on the channel. Run a read run. Expect the final code `0x15` on each channel. On SEC_DIS also expect `security_disable_o` = 1: the SEC_DIS go of the walk is the first SEC_DIS match of the leaf and the control of the revision-enable assumption, and a leaf in which `security_disable_o` stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. The code before this go is Idle, so the run sets `W_hash` and `n_idle` of the channel and grades no Idle-first rule. The first RMA channel of the walk is the control of the digest staging: a result other than `0x15` fails the leaf with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and the later steps are not graded.
.. Hold cell. Write token F to the channel with no go. Read the match register back to back through a window of a multiple of `W_hash` reads (the multiple is a test parameter). Expect every read to equal the code of the previous sub-step.
.. Idle-first cell. Go on the channel with the words of F. Run a read run. Expect the final code `0x2a`. The held code H is the code of the previous sub-step, `0x15` on every channel, so a go that has no effect leaves `0x15` and fails; CHK-TOKEN grades the run against H and `n_idle`. On SEC_DIS, log `security_disable_o` after the run.
. Mismatch walk. Run this step and the next once for each of the 18 legal (shape, channel) cells, in the drawn order. Write the words of the shape of the cell to the channel of the cell. The base token is `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_SIP, `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_CHIPLET and `TOKEN_ZERO` on SEC_DIS, and it is never presented unchanged. Flip: the base token with the bit at the drawn position inverted. Swap: two distinct words of the base token exchanged. Byte swap: one non-palindromic word of the base token byte-reversed. Zeros: eight zero words. Ones: eight all-ones words. Digest as token: the eight words that hold the expected digest of the channel (RMA: the staged digest; SEC_DIS: the digest constant bound at the bench), word k holding bits [32k+31:32k]. Foreign: the token F of the channel. Before the shape is written, set the held Match: write the base token unchanged to the channel, go and poll, and expect `0x15` (on SEC_DIS also `security_disable_o` = 1). This is the only unchanged presentation of the base token in the cell.
. Go on the channel of the cell. Run a read run. Expect the final code `0x2a`. The held code H is `0x15`, so a go that has no effect leaves `0x15` and fails; CHK-TOKEN grades the run against H and `n_idle`. For the digest-as-token shape, log the token words beside the digest value.
. Independence cell. Run this step and the next once for each channel, in the drawn order; that channel is the changed channel. Bring the three channels to non-Idle codes: write `TOKEN_T1` to RMA_SIP, go, poll (Match); write `TOKEN_OTHER` to RMA_CHIPLET, go, poll (Mismatch); write `TOKEN_OTHER` to SEC_DIS, go, poll (Mismatch), or, when SEC_DIS is the changed channel, write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go, poll (Match). Read the three match registers and log the held codes.
. Write to the changed channel the token that changes its verdict: `TOKEN_OTHER` on RMA_SIP and `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_CHIPLET; on SEC_DIS write `TOKEN_OTHER`, which changes the held Match to a Mismatch. Go on the changed channel. Poll the changed channel and expect the changed verdict, so a go that has no effect fails. Read the other two match registers at once, then through a window of a multiple of `W_hash` reads. Expect both to equal their held codes.
. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15`; this is the held code H. Write `TOKEN_T1` to SEC_DIS and go on SEC_DIS. Run a read run on SEC_DIS. Expect the final code `0x2a`; H is `0x15`, so a go that has no effect fails. Read the RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET match registers. Expect each to equal its code before this go.
. For each RMA channel that holds a Match, write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a`, so that no RMA match reaches the next cold reset by accident.
. Pre-sense cell. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15`. Expect `security_disable_o` = 1; a leaf in which `security_disable_o` stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Cold reset. The retained SEC_DIS match holds from reset release, so sensing does not start.
. Error injection. Point: `TOKEN_EOP` go on an RMA channel. Kind: a go before sense done. Expected response: the result of the channel stays `0x00` (read in the step that follows the wait). Write `TOKEN_T1` to RMA_SIP and go on RMA_SIP. Write `TOKEN_T2` to RMA_CHIPLET and go on RMA_CHIPLET.
. Write no SEC_DIS go, so that the retained SEC_DIS match holds sensing off. Poll the SEC_DIS match register until it reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15`. Wait a window of a multiple of the larger `W_hash` of the two RMA channels (the multiple is a test parameter) with the match held. Read the SEC_DIS match register. Expect `0x15`.
. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` immediately before and immediately after each read. Read the RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET match registers. Expect `0x00` on both. A read with sense done high at either sample does not count, and a leaf with no counted read of a channel fails.
. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. Before any new RMA go, poll the RMA_SIP and RMA_CHIPLET match registers until each reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15` on both. This is the control of the cell: the hash of the pre-sense go completed, and only the sensing gate held the result at `0x00`.
. Go on RMA_SIP and on RMA_CHIPLET again, with the arrays unchanged. Poll each. Expect `0x15` on both.
. Retention cell. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to RMA_CHIPLET, go and poll. Expect `0x2a`, so that only RMA_SIP holds a Match. Read the RMA_SIP match register. Expect `0x15`.
. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15` and `security_disable_o` = 1. Cold reset. The SEC_DIS match holds sensing off.
. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` immediately before and immediately after each read. Poll the SEC_DIS match register until it reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15`. Wait a window of a multiple of the larger `W_hash` of the two RMA channels with the match held. Read the RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS match registers. Expect `0x00`, `0x00` and `0x15`. Read the eight words of each of the three arrays. Expect the words last written: `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_OTHER` and `TOKEN_ZERO`. A read with sense done high at either sample does not count, and a leaf with no counted read fails.
. Write `TOKEN_OTHER` to SEC_DIS, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. With no new go, poll the RMA_SIP match register until it reads a value other than `0x00`. Expect `0x15`.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|The SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A wrong binding makes `TOKEN_ZERO` a mismatch token and can make `TOKEN_OTHER` the right one, so every SEC_DIS verdict of the leaf would rest on a false premise. Anchor: the bound digest constant at the bench against the golden digest. Control: the first SEC_DIS match of the leaf (the SEC_DIS go of the walk) raises `security_disable_o`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h>`.
|CHK-TOKEN
|The final code of each go (frontdoor readback of the match register) equals the SHA-256 golden: `0x15` for the right token, `0x2a` for each mismatch shape, token F and `TOKEN_OTHER`. The Idle observation is relative: in a read run after a go from a non-Idle held code H, no read after the first `0x00` returns H when H differs from the final code, no read returns a code outside {H, `0x00`, the final code}, and the run holds at least one `0x00` when `n_idle` of the first run of that channel is 2 or more (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: a start clears digest validity, so the comparison returns idle while the new digest is unavailable). The check never grades a single read at a fixed offset from the go. Every go starts from a held code other than its expected final code: Idle `0x00` at the first go of a channel, `0x15` before each mismatch go, and `0x2a` before the verdict-changing Match go of the independence cell. The held code is read before the go and logged as `held`, so a go that has no effect leaves the held code and fails. A DUT that accepts a digest as a token, swaps the word order, shares one result across channels or keeps the old code through a go whose hash outlasts the read period fails. Control: the right token returns `0x15` on each channel in the walk, and again on each RMA channel and on SEC_DIS in the pre-sense cell of the same leaf; the Idle-first grade applies only where the code before the go is non-Idle (the idle-first cell, the mismatch cell and the independence cell) and `n_idle` of the first run of the channel is 2 or more, and the first run of the channel from Idle measures `n_idle`. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> shape=<match\|other\|flip@<p>\|swap<i,j>\|bswap<i>\|zeros\|ones\|digest_as_token\|foreign> exp=0x<15\|2a> got=0x<v> held=0x<v\|na> idle_reads=<n> n_idle_ref=<n> idle_first_graded=<0\|1> [tok_eq_digest=1]`. A digest-as-token line logs the token words beside the digest value.
|CHK-TOKEN-CODES
|Every value read from a match register in the leaf is one of `0x00`, `0x15` and `0x2a` (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission, table Token result encodings; the Error code `0x3f` needs a comparator fault, which this test does not inject). A DUT that emits another 6-bit value fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-CODES PASS seed=<n> allowed=00,15,2a seen=<list> reads=<n>`.
|CHK-TOKEN-HOLD
|After a Match or a Mismatch, new word writes into the same channel array with no go leave the result unchanged: every back-to-back read through the window equals the held code (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: writing the token words alone does not invalidate the previous result or launch a comparison). A DUT that clears or recomputes the result on a word write shows `0x00` in the window and fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback. Control: the window is a multiple of the `W_hash` reads that the leaf measured at the go of the same channel, and the go after the window reaches a result inside `W_hash` reads. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-HOLD PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> held=0x<v> reads_in_window=<n> all_equal=1`.
|CHK-TOKEN-INDEP
|A go on one channel leaves the held results of the other two unchanged, at once and after the window, and `TOKEN_T1` on the SEC_DIS channel gives `0x2a`, because its constant is not the OTP digest (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A DUT with a shared result register or a shared comparison fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of all three match registers. Control: the held codes of the three channels are non-Idle before the go, the go channel changes its verdict (a go that has no effect fails), and the read run of the go channel follows the Idle rule of CHK-TOKEN. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-INDEP PASS seed=<n> go=<ch> held=0x<v>,0x<v> others=0x<v>,0x<v> sdis_t1=0x2a`.
|CHK-NORESET
|After the first sense of the leaf and before any token write, all 24 token word registers read X at the tap (`token_processing.adoc`, opening paragraph: the arrays have no reset). A DUT that resets the arrays fails. Control: the tap shows the first word written in the walk with its written value, so the tap is alive; the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` if it does not. Evidence: `CHK-NORESET PASS seed=<n> arrays=3 x_words=24 ctl_word=0x<w>`.
|CHK-TOKEN-RETAIN
|After a cold reset with the SEC_DIS match held and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` low at both samples of each read, once the SEC_DIS result has polled to non-Idle and a window of a multiple of `W_hash` has passed with the match held, the RMA results read `0x00`, the SEC_DIS result reads the code of its retained digest (`0x15`) and the 24 words equal the written words (`security_disable.adoc`, section Reset Persistence; `token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission). After sense done the retained RMA_SIP digest, polled to non-Idle with no new go, gives `0x15`. A DUT that clears a digest or an array on reset fails. A read with sense done high at either sample does not count. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` port. Control: the pre-reset code of RMA_SIP is `0x15` and the SEC_DIS match raised `security_disable_o`. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-RETAIN PASS seed=<n> pre=0x15 sense_done_pre=0 sense_done_post=0 post_reset_rma=0x00 post_reset_sdis=0x15 words_equal=24 post_sense=0x15`.
|CHK-TOKEN-PRESENSE
|With `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` low at both samples of each read, a go on each RMA channel reads `0x00` after the retained SEC_DIS match has polled to `0x15` and a window of a multiple of `W_hash` has passed with the match held (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures: RMA results stay idle until fuse sensing completes). A DUT that compares before sensing shows `0x15` or `0x2a` once the hash completes, so it fails; the window is a multiple of the `W_hash` that the leaf measured at the walk go of the same channel. Control on the same channel: after the SEC_DIS mismatch, the cold reset and sense done, and before any new RMA go, the result polls to `0x15` from the digest that the pre-sense go computed, so the hash completed and only the sensing gate held the result at `0x00`; the leaf fails if it does not. The same go made again after sense done reads `0x15`. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the port. Evidence: `CHK-TOKEN-PRESENSE PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp> sense_done=0 sdis_held=0x15 window=<n> pre=0x00 retained_post_sense=0x15 post=0x15`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not write two go bits in one `TOKEN_EOP` write, does not grade the digest word and byte order in the OTP shadow (the staging is a stimulus choice that the first Match step controls), does not present a token with `secure_tm_req_i` at 1, and does not inject a comparator fault; the fault matrix test owns the error code `0x3f`. A match does not update the state shadow, because `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` is 0. The open tests `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`, `sep_sec_dis_override_test` and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test` keep the cells that they grade.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test]]
===== 6.24 `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 3 seeds; every leaf runs one drawn fault cell on each of the three channels, each ended by a cold reset (the cold reset clears the sticky flags; the image, the token arrays and the digests stay), and the two common-mode cells and the three freeze cells, which set no sticky flag and take no cold reset, so a leaf takes three cold resets after its first sense; tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. The PIC source 40 request line is read as a probe, so no CPU model is needed.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`). The OTP bank model preload. Injects, approved: `token_cmp_fault_inject_i` with `token_cmp_fault_sel_i` (copy 0 of each channel only) and `token_digest_test_en_inject_i` (the RMA_SIP digest latch only). Probes, approved: the PIC source 40 request line (`sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o[39]`), `security_disable_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the RMA_SIP digest latch (`token_digest_*`). Added hooks, error injects that the test adds to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`: the comparator copy select at the `token_cmp_fault` injection port (copy 1 and copy 2 cells) and the test-enable inject of the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS digest latches (their freeze cells). The digest latch probes of RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS and the comparator rail taps `cmp_match_p_i` and `cmp_match_n_i` are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The test computes the SHA-256 golden of each token.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The cell list: cells 1 to 18 are the fault cells (channel {RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET, SEC_DIS} x kind {collapse, disagree} x copy {0, 1, 2}); cells 19 and 20 are the common-mode cells (held Match, held Mismatch) on RMA_CHIPLET; cells 21 to 23 are the freeze cells of RMA_SIP, RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS. Every leaf draws one fault cell per channel, with equal weight among the new cells of the channel: on RMA_SIP the four cells of copies 1 and 2 (collapse or disagree); on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS those four and the copy-0 disagree cell. The copy-0 collapse cells and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_SIP are not drawn. On an RMA channel the held phase of the fault cell (Match or Mismatch) is drawn with equal weight; the SEC_DIS channel holds a Mismatch only. Every leaf also runs the cells 19 to 23, which need no cold reset. Also drawn: the order of the eight cells of the leaf, the word-write order of every array write and the disable-vector pair. Trap filters: inject only while the held result is non-Idle; release before the next comparison; never hold a SEC_DIS match while an injection is active; no common-mode flip on SEC_DIS (a flip of a SEC_DIS mismatch is a SEC_DIS match). `reseed = 3` gives three fault cells per channel per regression. The fault cells and their held phases are seed-dependent and close over a merge of regressions: the five fault cells of RMA_CHIPLET or SEC_DIS in about 3 regressions (5 ln 5 / 3), and the ten cells (fault cell and held phase) of RMA_CHIPLET in about 8 (10 ln 10 / 3). Every leaf has a new fault cell on each channel, and the cells 19 to 23 run on every seed.

*Objective*: A collapsed differential pair or a disagreeing comparator copy, injected while a comparison is valid, forces the result `0x3f`, sets the sticky flag of that channel (bit 0, 8 or 16 of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`) and the PIC source 40 request. The flags have no write-one-to-clear, survive a later valid comparison and clear only on reset. A common-mode flip with legal pairs raises nothing and inverts the held result. A test enable at the digest latch of a channel freezes the retained digest and its valid bit, so a later token operation on that channel changes neither (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures: test enable freezes those retained latches). The specification names no test-enable signal and states no effect on a match in progress (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Test enable and token freeze), so the freeze cells grade the two latches only and grade no result code under the freeze. No step writes `LC_STATE` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission, Matching Logic Countermeasures); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (register `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (section SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map, row Token match fault).

*Test Coverage*: The 14 new fault cells (collapse and disagree at comparator copies 1 and 2 on each channel, and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_CHIPLET and of SEC_DIS), one drawn per channel per leaf, each in a drawn held-Match or held-Mismatch phase on the RMA channels and a held-Mismatch phase on SEC_DIS, all 14 over a merge of regressions. The common-mode flip of a held Match and of a held Mismatch on RMA_CHIPLET. The sticky flag over a valid retry, the absence of a write-one-to-clear, the clear by cold reset (the token words are read and logged), and the absence of a SEC_DIS match under a fault. The freeze of the retained digest and valid bit on each of the three channels. The test extends `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`, `sep_efuse_token_match_fault_pic_test` and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test`. This test grades only the new cells: the comparator copies 1 and 2 on every channel, the disagreeing copy on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS, the common-mode flip with the exact flipped code on RMA_CHIPLET, the held-Match and held-Mismatch phases of each fault, the absence of a SEC_DIS match under a fault, and the freeze of the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS latches. The freeze cell of RMA_SIP is a control: it runs on every seed, a failing control fails the leaf, and it is not credited as new coverage. The copy-0 collapse cells of every channel and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_SIP are not drawn: the open tests grade them. The open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_token_fault_cg` (every coverpoint; `x_ch_kind_copy` and `x_ch_retry` are seed-dependent), `sep_token_channel_cg` (`cp_code`, bin `0x3f`) and `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg` (`x_verdict_out`, Error cells).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the fault cell of each channel (kind, copy and, on an RMA channel, held phase), the order of the eight cells of the leaf, the word orders and the disable-vector pair. Print `SEED seed=<n>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Run steps 3 to 13 once per cell, in the drawn order, with only the steps of the kind of the cell. After the last step of each cell, a channel that holds a Match takes `TOKEN_OTHER`, a go and a poll (expect `0x2a`). A fault cell then ends with the cold reset of step 10. A common-mode cell or a freeze cell sets no sticky flag and takes no cold reset. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. "Poll c" reads the match register of channel c until it reads a value other than `0x00`. "Go on c" writes 1 to the go bit of c in `TOKEN_EOP` (bit 0 RMA_SIP, bit 8 RMA_CHIPLET, bit 16 SEC_DIS) with no other bit set. Every go on SEC_DIS runs with `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Stage the OTP image: TEST_DEV; the digest of `TOKEN_T1` in `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` and that of `TOKEN_T2` in `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` (word k holding bits [32k+31:32k]); `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0, so that no held or flipped match updates the state shadow; every other 256-bit field at its fixed vector; a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits. Run `CHK-BIND`. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. SEC_DIS control step. Before each cell that uses the SEC_DIS channel (a fault cell or the freeze cell), first write `TOKEN_ZERO` to the SEC_DIS array, go on SEC_DIS and poll. Expect `0x15` and `security_disable_o` = 1 (control of the revision-enable assumption: a leaf in which the probe stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, and it is also the control of the "never" check on `security_disable_o` in step 7). Write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a` and `security_disable_o` = 0, so that no SEC_DIS match is held when the injection starts.
. Fault and common-mode cells (cells 1 to 20). Create the held result on the channel of the cell: on an RMA channel write the right token (`TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2`) for a held Match or `TOKEN_OTHER` for a held Mismatch; on SEC_DIS write `TOKEN_OTHER`. Go. Poll. Read the held code.
. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, the PIC source 40 request line and `security_disable_o`. Expect 0, 0 and 0 (the control that the flags and the request are low before the injection).
. Error injection. Point: `token_cmp_fault_inject_i` with `token_cmp_fault_sel_i` at the channel and copy of the cell. Kind: pair collapse (both rails of the copy at the same value), disagreement of one copy, or unanimous legal flip (common-mode cells, no copy axis). Expected response: as in the Objective. Apply the injection only while the held result is non-Idle. In a collapse or disagree cell, the comparator copy select routes the injection to the copy of the cell. Hook control: while the injection is active, the comparator rail taps `cmp_match_p_i` and `cmp_match_n_i` show the injected kind on the copy of the cell only, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. A collapse cell shows the collapsed pair on the copy of the cell and legal pairs on the other two copies. A disagree cell shows legal pairs on all three copies, and the copy of the cell is the minority copy, whose pair differs from the equal pairs of the other two copies. The copy-0 disagree cell uses the existing inject, which sets copy 0 to the Mismatch pair and copies 1 and 2 to the Match pair in either held phase, so the hook control of that cell expects exactly that rail pattern.
. Read the match register of the channel until it differs from the held code (bounded). Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, the PIC source 40 request line and `security_disable_o`. Expect, for a collapse or disagree cell, `0x3f`, the flag of that channel only, the request at 1, and `security_disable_o` at 0. Expect, for a common-mode cell, the flipped code (`0x2a` for a held Match, `0x15` for a held Mismatch), no flag and the request at 0.
. Release the injection. For a collapse or disagree cell: read the match register and log it, then make the retry with the token of the opposite verdict: the right token (`TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2`, or `TOKEN_ZERO` on SEC_DIS) after a held Mismatch, `TOKEN_OTHER` after a held Match. Go and poll. Expect the opposite verdict (`0x15` after a held Mismatch, `0x2a` after a held Match). The comparator result can return to the held verdict when the injection is released, so only a verdict change shows that the retry comparison ran. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC source 40 request line. Expect the flag and the request still set. A common-mode cell ends here and logs the N/A lines of steps 9 and 10.
. Error injection. Point: frontdoor write of `0x0001_0101` to `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` (a 1 in each fault bit). Kind: write to a read-only register. Expected response: the flags and the request are unchanged; the response code is not graded. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC source 40 request line.
. For a channel that holds a Match, write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a`. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC source 40 request line. Expect 0 and 0. Read the eight words of the array of the channel and log them. They are not graded here.
. Freeze cells (cells 21 to 23), on the channel of the cell. Token A is `TOKEN_OTHER`. Token B is `TOKEN_T2` on RMA_SIP and `TOKEN_T1` on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS. Both are mismatch tokens on their channel, so no match is held. Write token B, go and poll. Count the polls as `W_hash`. Read the digest latch and its valid bit as L_B. Expect the valid bit at 1. Write token A, go and poll. Read the digest latch and its valid bit as L_A. Expect the valid bit at 1 and L_A different from L_B. The specification gives the comparison equation and states no word or byte layout of the latch, so the leaf compares latch values with each other and never with a value computed from a token. The RMA_SIP cell uses the existing probes `token_digest_*`, and the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS cells read their latch probes (observation taps).
. Error injection. Point: the test enable of the digest latch of the channel (`token_digest_test_en_inject_i` on RMA_SIP, the added test-enable inject on RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS). Kind: a test enable that freezes the retained latches. Expected response: the sticky digest and its valid bit keep their values through a token operation. Assert the test enable. Write token B to the channel and go. Expect the word writes and the go to answer OKAY and the array to read back token B. Wait a window of a multiple of `W_hash` (the multiple is a test parameter). Read the digest latch and its valid bit. Expect L_A and the valid bit of step 11.
. Release the test enable. Go on the channel again with token B in the array. Poll the digest latch and its valid bit until the valid bit is 1 and the digest differs from the frozen value, inside the same window. Expect L_B.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|Runs once per leaf. The SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A wrong binding would make `TOKEN_OTHER` an unreliable mismatch token. Anchor: the bound digest constant at the bench against the golden digest. Control: the SEC_DIS match of the SEC_DIS control step (step 3) raises `security_disable_o`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h>`.
|CHK-FAULT-CELL
|The result code (frontdoor readback), `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` (frontdoor readback), the PIC source 40 request line and `security_disable_o` (probes) equal the golden of the cell. A collapse or disagree cell gives `0x3f`, the flag of that channel only (bit 0, 8 or 16) and the request at 1. A common-mode cell gives the flipped code of the held code (`0x2a` for a held Match, `0x15` for a held Mismatch) with no flag and no request, so a no-op injection leaves the held code and fails (`token_processing.adoc`, section Matching Logic Countermeasures). On the SEC_DIS channel `security_disable_o` stays 0 under the fault, because `0x3f` does not satisfy a match. A DUT that raises no flag, sets the wrong channel flag or raises a fault for a legal flip fails. Control: the injection is applied only while the held result is non-Idle, the read before the injection shows the flags and the request at 0, and the SEC_DIS-channel cells show `security_disable_o` at 1 for the match of step 3 (the control of the "never" check). Hook control of a collapse or disagree cell: while the injection is active, the comparator rail taps show the injected kind on the copy of the cell only (a collapsed pair on that copy, or that copy as the minority copy of a disagreement; the copy-0 disagree cell expects copy 0 at Mismatch and copies 1 and 2 at Match in either held phase), else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, so a copy select that misroutes the fault cannot pass a copy 1 or copy 2 cell. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-CELL PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> kind=<collapse\|disagree\|cm_match\|cm_mismatch> copy=<0\|1\|2\|na> rail_copy=<0\|1\|2\|na> role=new held=0x<v> res=0x<v> exp=0x<v> flags=0x<v> exp_flags=0x<v> pic40=<0\|1> secdis=<0\|1> others=0x0`.
|CHK-FAULT-STICKY
|Collapse and disagree cells. After the release, the retry with the token of the opposite verdict returns that verdict (`0x2a` after a held Match, `0x15` after a held Mismatch) and the flag and the request stay set (`token_processing.adoc`, section Matching Logic Countermeasures: a later valid comparison can report match or mismatch while the flag and the interrupt remain asserted). A DUT that clears the flag on a valid retry fails. A retry whose go has no effect leaves the held verdict and fails, because the retry verdict differs from the held verdict. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the PIC probe. Control: the held verdict before the injection and the flag at 1 after the injection, so the retry starts from a set flag. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-STICKY PASS seed=<n> held=0x<v> pre_retry=0x<v> retry_exp=0x<v> retry_res=0x<v> flags=0x<v> pic40=1`. A common-mode cell logs `CHK-FAULT-STICKY N/A seed=<n> kind=<cm_match\|cm_mismatch>`.
|CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C
|Collapse and disagree cells. A write of 1 to each fault bit leaves the flags unchanged and the request at 1 (`token_processing.adoc`, section Matching Logic Countermeasures: there is no write-one-to-clear operation to deassert the source). A DUT that clears a flag on a write of 1 fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` and the PIC probe. Control: the flag of the channel reads 1 before the write. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C PASS seed=<n> wr=0x10101 flags_before=0x<v> flags=0x<v> pic40=1`. A common-mode cell logs `CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C N/A seed=<n> kind=<cm_match\|cm_mismatch>`.
|CHK-FAULT-RESET
|Collapse and disagree cells. After a cold reset and sense done the flags and the request are 0 (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Submission: reset clears the sticky fault flags). The retention of the token arrays over reset belongs to `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test`; the words are read and logged here and are not graded. A DUT that keeps a flag over reset fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the PIC probe. Control: the flag and the request are 1 before the reset, and the leaf has ended any held Match with a mismatch go before the reset. Evidence: `CHK-FAULT-RESET PASS seed=<n> flags=0x0 pic40=0 words_logged=1`. A common-mode cell logs `CHK-FAULT-RESET N/A seed=<n> kind=<cm_match\|cm_mismatch>`.
|CHK-LATCH-FREEZE
|Freeze cells. With the test enable asserted at the latch of a channel, the sticky digest and its valid bit after the token operation equal their values before it (L_A), and after the release the token operation replaces the digest with L_B, the value that the same token B gave in a go without the freeze (`token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures: test enable freezes the retained latches). The comparison is relative, because the specification states no layout of the latch. A DUT whose latch takes the new digest or clears the valid bit under the freeze fails. No result code is graded under the freeze. Anchor: the digest latch probes. Control: the digests of the two non-frozen operations differ (L_A is not L_B, so the probe is alive and tells the two tokens apart), the word writes and the go under the freeze answer OKAY with token B read back, and the release operation replaces the digest inside the same window, so the freeze window is long enough for a change to show. The RMA_SIP cell is a control of the leg (`role=control`), and the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS cells are new (`role=new`). Evidence: `CHK-LATCH-FREEZE PASS seed=<n> ch=<sip\|chp\|sdis> role=<control\|new> digest_before=0x<h> digest_frozen=0x<h> valid_before=<0\|1> valid_frozen=<0\|1> digest_after_release=0x<h> digest_b=0x<h>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: For every cell of the leaf, every checker that the cell requires PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0. `CHK-BIND` runs once per leaf. A cell requires: in a fault cell (cells 1 to 18), `CHK-FAULT-CELL`, `CHK-FAULT-STICKY`, `CHK-FAULT-NO-W1C` and `CHK-FAULT-RESET`; in a common-mode cell (cells 19 and 20), `CHK-FAULT-CELL` only; in a freeze cell (cells 21 to 23), `CHK-LATCH-FREEZE`. The N/A lines that a checker defines for a common-mode cell are the required output of that checker in that cell and are not a miss. A checker that a cell does not require logs no line for that cell. A leaf whose hook control fails fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.

*Scope limit*: The test grades no result code under the freeze and no match in progress under a test enable, because the specification states neither. It makes no SEC_DIS match while an injection is active. It does not write `LC_STATE`, and a match does not update the state shadow, because `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` is 0. It does not grade the result of a common-mode cell after the release. The open tests `sep_efuse_rma_token_rand_test`, `sep_efuse_token_match_fault_pic_test` and `sep_efuse_digest_latch_fault_test` keep the cells that they grade. The RMA_SIP freeze cell is a control here, and the copy-0 collapse cells and the copy-0 disagree cell of RMA_SIP are not drawn. The CPU and PIC delivery of source 40 belongs to `sep_efuse_token_match_fault_pic_test`.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test]]
===== 6.25 `sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`). The OTP bank model with its image preload. The port `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. The read and program interface outputs (state, error, done, busy, and the request valid before the guard). No inject. The OTP bank probe `otp_bank_probe_o` (snapshot, bit diff, and a sample of the image on every clock cycle) and the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The bank probe is the anchor of every statement about a bank bit, because the read interface is under test here and the direct read is no anchor. The command tap is the anchor of the read-command statements of CHK-IF-CMD and of the program-command statement of CHK-IF-PRESENSE.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: the case order (all eight cases run on every seed), the out-of-range address (8192, 65535 and one address drawn in 8193 to 65534), the slot of the burned write-lock, the slot of the burned read-lock, and the spare fields and zero bits of the programs. Both lock slots are data slots from 1 to 39, and the read-lock slot is none of 11, 13 and 15. Trap filters: an out-of-range address is never 8191 or lower; slot 0 (`LC_STATE`, which ignores both lock bits), slots 41 to 47 (unassigned) and slot 40 (the field of bit address 8191) are never a lock slot; the two lock slots, the spare fields of the twins and the pre-sense program are pairwise different; the field of the case-frame read has no burned lock bit; every program targets a bit that is 0 in the image, and the field of the write-lock slot has such a bit in its used range. `reseed = 4` samples other case orders, other out-of-range addresses, other lock slots and other spare bits. No bin depends on a drawn address, slot or bit, so later regressions add samples and no cell waits on them. The eight cases are cheap register-level cases with their error injection, and they run on every seed.

*Objective*: A read go with `read_enable` clear sets `read_status`. A read or program address of 8192 or more sets its own address-error flag, and `read_status` for a read, and the flag clears by write-one. A request that a lock blocks sets `efuse_req_error`. `program_enable` keeps a read from reaching the bank. No bank bit changes from reset release until sense completes, because the command multiplexer gives sensing priority until it completes. A program with data 0 programs nothing. Sources: `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Command-path ownership: software program and read requests share one command path to the OTP bank after sense completes); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (the opening section, sentence on the command multiplexer: automatic sensing priority until it completes; sections Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control; Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization, for the write-lock of each field only). The OTP has 256 words of 32 bits, so 8192 bits (`hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`, total storage capacity; `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Fields). The specification states only the status flags for an out-of-range address and not whether the guard drops the command, so the image after an out-of-range request is logged and not graded, and so is the bank command at the post-guard tap (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The data returned by a lock-blocked read is not graded (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).

*Test Coverage*: Eight cases on every seed: a program go before sense done; a read go without enable; an out-of-range read at 8192, 65535 and a drawn address, after an accepted read of bit address 8191; an out-of-range program at the same three addresses; a program on a field with a burned write-lock; a read on a field with a burned read-lock; a read go with both enables set; a program with data 0. Each refusal case has an accepted twin in the same leaf. The flag clear by write-one for each address-error flag. The test extends `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test` (programs of unlocked and write-locked spare fields, image check after resense) and `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test` (the clear of a read error by a successful read and by a fresh legal request). This test grades only the new cells: the set of `read_status` by a go without enable, the status flags of each illegal request, the absence of a bank change for the data-0 and pre-sense programs, and the out-of-range flags at 8192, 65535 and a drawn address with the accepted read of bit 8191. The lock-blocked program grades its `efuse_req_error` flag and its accepted twin; its image result is logged, because `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test` and `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test` grade the unchanged bank bit of a locked field. The open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_if_status_cg` (every coverpoint except `cp_sense_status_word` and `x_prog_lifecycle`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the case order, the out-of-range address, the two lock slots and the spare bits from the seed. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. A bit address is 8 times the shadow byte offset of the field plus the bit index. W is a multiple of the sense time that the leaf measures at step 3 (the multiple is a test parameter). Nothing shows that a busy bit rises before the first poll, so no wait ends on a busy bit. "Run a program" writes `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable`, `efuse_data`, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_program_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, then reads the status registers and requires `program_busy` and `read_busy` at 0. "Run a read" writes `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable`, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_read_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, then reads the status registers and requires `program_busy` and `read_busy` at 0.
. Stage the OTP image: TEST_DEV; every 256-bit field at its fixed vector; a burned write-lock for the slot of the lock-blocked program and a burned read-lock for the slot of the lock-blocked read; no other lock bit; narrow fields at staged values that have at least one 0 in their used bits; a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits.
. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time from reset release to the rise.
. Case frame. Before every case except the pre-sense case: write 0 to `program_enable`, `read_enable` and both go bits; write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`; run a read of an unlocked in-range bit with `read_enable` = 1; read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`; expect the three error flags and `read_status` at 0; take a snapshot of the OTP bank at `otp_bank_probe_o`.
. Run the eight cases of steps 6 to 14 in the drawn order; step 7 follows step 6 at once. After each case, except the pre-sense case and the both-enables case, which state their own reads, read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, and take the bank diff against the snapshot at `otp_bank_probe_o`.
. Pre-sense case. Cold reset. The leaf has one t=0 image, and every program of an earlier case stays in the bank. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go. Kind: a go of a program before sense done. Expected response: no bank bit changes from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises; the flags are logged and not graded. As the first access after the reset release, write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` in one write with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, the address of an unlocked spare bit that no earlier case of the leaf programmed, and `efuse_program_go` = 1. This case does not wait for the window W. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` at the completion of the go write. Expect low; if it is high, the leaf fails with `CHK-IF-PRESENSE FAIL` (the case did not run before sense done). At `otp_bank_probe_o`, take the bank image at reset release and compare it on every clock cycle from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises. Log `t_go` (cycles from reset release to the completion of the go write) and `t_sense` (cycles from reset release to the rise). Log the flags and do not grade them. Cold reset again, so that no held request carries over. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise.
. Accepted twin of the pre-sense case. After sense done, run a program of another unlocked spare bit.
