Expect `program_done` 1 and the flags at 0, and the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` to change by exactly that bit. Record the program command of the twin at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Log `t_land`, the number of cycles from the completion of the go write to the first cycle in which the bank image differs from its snapshot. If `t_go` + `t_land` is not shorter than `t_sense`, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, because a program that a wrong DUT lets through at the go would land after the rise and the pre-sense check could not fail.
. Read go without enable. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go. Kind: a read go with `read_enable` = 0 at an unlocked in-range bit. Expected response: `read_status` = 1 and the three error flags at 0. Run the read. Expect `read_status` = 1 and the three error flags at 0. Accepted twin: run a read of the same bit with `read_enable` = 1. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0.
. Out-of-range read. Run the case frame, then a read of bit address 8191 with `read_enable` = 1. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0 (the accepted twin). Then, for each address a in {8192, 65535, the drawn address}, run the case frame. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go. Kind: a read at the out-of-range address a with `read_enable` = 1. Expected response: `efuse_read_addr_error` = 1, `read_status` = 1, and the other two flags at 0. Run the read. Expect `efuse_read_addr_error` = 1, `read_status` = 1, and `efuse_program_addr_error` and `efuse_req_error` at 0. Log the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Write 1 to `efuse_read_addr_error_clear`. Expect `efuse_read_addr_error` = 0 and the other two flags unchanged.
. Out-of-range program. For each address a in {8192, 65535, the drawn address}, run the case frame. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go. Kind: a program at the out-of-range address a with `efuse_data` = 1 and `program_enable` = 1. Expected response: `efuse_program_addr_error` = 1, and the other two flags at 0. Run the program. Expect `efuse_program_addr_error` = 1, and `efuse_read_addr_error` and `efuse_req_error` at 0. Log the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Write 1 to `efuse_program_addr_error_clear`. Expect `efuse_program_addr_error` = 0 and the other two flags unchanged.
. Lock-blocked program. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go on the field with the burned write-lock. Kind: a request that a lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1 and the other two flags at 0. Run a program of a zero bit of that field. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1 and the other two flags at 0. Log the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`; it is not graded here. Write 1 to `efuse_req_error_clear`. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 0, the other two flags at 0 and `read_status` at 0. Accepted twin: run a program of a zero bit of an unlocked spare field. Expect `program_done` 1 and the flags at 0, and the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` to change by exactly that bit.
. Lock-blocked read. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go on the field with the burned read-lock. Kind: a request that a lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1; the data and the other flags are not graded. Run a read of a bit of that field. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1. Do not grade the data or the other flags. Write 1 to `efuse_req_error_clear`. Accepted twin: run a read of a bit of an unlocked field. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0.
. Both enables. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go while `program_enable` is set. Kind: a read request with both enables set. Expected response: no read command reaches the bank at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; the flags and `read_status` are logged and not graded. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1 and no go bit. Write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 1, an unlocked in-range bit and `efuse_read_go` in one write. Do not wait for `read_busy`, because the specification does not say that this read completes. After a wait of a test-parameter number of cycles, read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, log them and do not grade them. Write 0 to `program_enable` and `read_enable`. Cold reset, so that no held read carries over. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Run the case frame. Accepted twin: with `program_enable` = 0 and `read_enable` = 1, run the same read. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and the flags at 0.
. Data 0. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go. Kind: a program with `efuse_data` = 0 and `program_enable` = 1. Expected response: no bank change; the flags are logged and not graded. Run a program of a zero bit of an unlocked spare field with these values. Expect the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` unchanged. Log the flags and do not grade them. Accepted twin: run a program of the same bit with `efuse_data` = 1. Expect `program_done` 1 and the flags at 0, and the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` to change by exactly that bit.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-IF-STATUS
|After each case the error flags of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` and `read_status` of `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` equal the golden of the case (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`): a read go without enable sets `read_status` only; an out-of-range read sets `efuse_read_addr_error` and `read_status`; an out-of-range program sets `efuse_program_addr_error` only; a lock-blocked program sets `efuse_req_error` only; a lock-blocked read sets `efuse_req_error`. Each address-error flag clears by its write-one clear bit with the other flags unchanged, and in the lock-blocked program case `efuse_req_error` clears by `efuse_req_error_clear` with the other flags unchanged. A DUT that raises the wrong flag, sets a second flag or does not clear fails. The flags of the pre-sense, data-0 and both-enables cases and the other flags of the lock-blocked read are logged and not graded. Anchor: frontdoor readback of the status registers. Control: CHK-IF-CTRL (the flags read 0 before the case, and the accepted twin leaves them at 0). Evidence: `CHK-IF-STATUS PASS seed=<n> case=<name> addr=<a> status=0b<rs,rae,pae,re> exp=0b<rs,rae,pae,re> cleared=<1\|na>`, where status is {`read_status`, `efuse_read_addr_error`, `efuse_program_addr_error`, `efuse_req_error`}.
|CHK-IF-CMD
|The OTP image is unchanged after the data-0 program (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, field `efuse_data`: a program with data 0 is ignored). A DUT that lets an empty program change a bank bit fails. Anchor: the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Control: CHK-IF-CTRL, because the accepted twin of the same leaf changes the image by exactly its bit. For the out-of-range cases and the lock-blocked program the image diff is logged only, and the lock-blocked program grades only its flag in CHK-IF-STATUS. No read command reaches the bank port (anchor: `efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the go to the end of W for the lock-blocked read, and from the go to the assertion of the cold reset for the read under both enables; the sense after that cold reset is outside the window. Control on the same tap: the accepted read twin of each of these two cases shows one read command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; a missing command fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-IF-CMD PASS seed=<n> case=prog_data0 otp_diff=none`; `CHK-IF-CMD PASS seed=<n> case=<read_rlocked\|both_enables> read_cmds=0 ctl_twin_cmd=1`; logged cases: `CHK-IF-CMD INFO seed=<n> case=<read_oob\|prog_oob\|prog_wlocked> addr=<a> otp_diff=<none\|a>`.
|CHK-IF-CTRL
|Each refusal case has an accepted twin in the same leaf that completes: a program twin ends with `program_done` high, `program_busy` low and the flags at 0 after the window W, and changes the image at `otp_bank_probe_o` by exactly its bit; a read twin ends with `read_done` high, `read_status` low and the flags at 0. The three error flags and `read_status` read 0 before every case. A leaf that lacks a twin or a clean start fails, because a refusal check without its control cannot show that the interface works. Anchor: frontdoor readback and the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. Evidence: `CHK-IF-CTRL PASS seed=<n> case=<name> accepted=1 flags=0x0 landed=<1\|na> read_done=<1\|na>`.
|CHK-IF-PRESENSE
|From reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises the OTP image has no changed bit, with `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` low at the program go (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, opening section: the command multiplexer gives automatic sensing priority until it completes; `architecture.adoc`, section Command-path ownership: software program and read requests share one command path to the bank after sense completes). A DUT that lets the program reach the bank during sensing fails. The image after sense done is not graded, because the specification does not say whether a held request runs later. Anchor: the OTP bank image on every clock cycle from reset release to the rise of the port, against the image at reset release (`otp_bank_probe_o`), and the port sampled at the go. Control: the accepted twin of step 7, a program of another spare bit after sense done, lands in the image, and the leaf logs `t_go`, `t_sense` and `t_land` of the twin; the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL` when `t_go` + `t_land` is not shorter than `t_sense`, so that a program let through at the go would land before the rise. From reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises, no program command reaches `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; the read commands of the sense are allowed. Control on the same tap: the accepted twin of step 7 shows one program command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`; a missing command fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-IF-PRESENSE PASS seed=<n> sense_done_at_go=0 otp_diff_at_sense_done=none ctl_diff=<a> t_go=<n> t_land=<n> t_sense=<n> prog_cmds_presense=0 ctl_cmd=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not grade the image after an out-of-range request or after the lock-blocked program, the status flags of the pre-sense, data-0 and both-enables cases, the data of a lock-blocked read or the bank command at the post-guard port after an out-of-range request. The clear of `read_status` by a successful read and by a fresh legal request belongs to `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test`, and the unlocked and write-locked spare programs after resense belong to `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`. The read timeout and the program timeout paths belong to `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test`.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 6.26 `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. A leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the leaf kind and its image, with the lock layout (the preload hook stages it from the seed).

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`), including `FEAT_CTRL` read as `SEP_LIFECYCLE_CTRL.FEAT_CTRL`. The sensed-shadow output `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. The OTP bank model with its image preload. The read and program interface outputs, including the `efuse_req_error` flag. No inject. The OTP bank probe `otp_bank_probe_o` (snapshot and bit diff) and the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The bank probe is the anchor of the bank statements of CHK-LOCK-CELL and CHK-PROG-ROUND, because a read-locked field has no direct read. The command tap is the anchor of the post-guard statement of CHK-LOCK-CELL.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Each leaf has one t=0 image, so the seed draws the leaf kind and the image of that kind. Leaf kinds and weights: the lock leg 4, the single-bit image 1, the exempt cell 1, the order cell 2 and the chain leg 2. Two classes. The lock leg, the single-bit image and the order cell are RAND-REP. The lock leg draws the lock layout of image A: the target slot (one of slots 3 to 40 with equal weight), which takes the lock kind write and read; a lock kind {none, write, read, write and read} with equal weight for every other data slot of 3 to 40, except that both neighbours of the target slot stay unlocked; three program slots of 3 to 40, other than the target slot and its neighbours; the bit positions; and the shadow-write bits. The single-bit image draws the slot (1 or 2) and the kind (read-lock or write-lock). The order cell draws the slot (3, 4 or 22) and the permutation of the three actions (one of the 6). Trap filters for these: no lock on a slot that the leaf needs later in the same image; in image A slots 1 and 2 carry no lock bit (their lock cells run in the single-bit image); the kind read alone is never drawn on slots 11, 13 and 15; the staged value of a read-locked field is non-zero in every word, so that the sensed-shadow compare shows the fill; in image C the staged `REQUIRED_ALGS` value has at least three used bits at 0, so that a, b and c are distinct free bits of slot 22; no reserved or unassigned bit. The chain leg is RANDCFG: round count 1 to 4, field, bit, read-back flag, version field {`BL1_VERSION`, `BL2_VERSION`} and burned index class {0, 255, a middle value in 1 to 254}, each with equal weight. Trap filters for the chain leg: data 1 only; the drawn bit is 0 in the image; the unlocked rounds never draw the version field of the version round; no program before sense done; no reserved or unassigned bit. `reseed = 4` gives four leaves per regression. Every cell of this test is seed-dependent and closes over a merge of regressions. A lock leaf reads every data slot of 1 to 40 under its drawn layout, so the read cells of the 40 slots close in about 4 regressions. A lock leaf programs about five slots (the target slot, its neighbours and the program slots), so the 76 program cells (slot 3 to 40, write-lock 0 or 1) close in about 45 regressions. The 6 cells of `x_version_bit` close in about 18 (6 ln 6 / 0.8 chain leaves per regression), the four single-bit cells in about 20, the six order permutations in about 18, and the exempt cell in about 3.

*Objective*: Slot n has its write-lock at `LOCKS` bit 2n and its read-lock at bit 2n+1 (slots 32 to 40 in `LOCKS_SPARE` bits 2(n-32) and 2(n-32)+1). A write-lock stops programming of its field and shadow writes where the field takes them. A read-lock stops the shadow read. The sensing state machine still fills a locked shadow. One slot never locks another field. `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE` and `LC_STATE` stay readable with every assigned lock bit burned. A programmed bit reaches the shadow only at the next sense (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection). A burned bit never clears across senses, and the result is the OR of all rounds. A burned `BL1_VERSION` or `BL2_VERSION` flag stays set. No step writes `LC_STATE` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); the program of its gated bits belongs to `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control, Fuse Programming and RMA Authorization, Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection, Fuse Fields, lock slot statement and field table, Anti-Rollback Security-Version Flags); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (registers `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, fields `program_enable`, `efuse_program_go`, `efuse_data`; `EFUSE_READ_CTRL`, fields `read_enable`, `efuse_read_go`, `read_done`, `read_status`; `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`). A read-lock also blocks a read-interface request on the field, and the blocked request sets `efuse_req_error` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, register `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`: the eFuse was blocked because of a read or a program lock). The specification does not state the response or the data of a frontdoor shadow read that a read-lock blocks, so the test logs them and does not grade them (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>, Result of a blocked read).

*Test Coverage*: The lock bit positions of slots 1 to 40 under a drawn lock layout, with the target slot locked for write and read and every other data slot of 3 to 40 at a drawn lock kind. The read-lock of each read-locked slot, graded by the `efuse_req_error` flag of a read-interface request on the field, with an accepted request on the unlocked neighbours of the target slot (`sep_efuse_interface_status_rand_test` grades the status flags of one lock-blocked read). The unlocked neighbours of the target slot. The sense that ignores locks. The single-bit fields (`SBOOT_DIS` slot 1, `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` slot 2) in a read-lock image and a write-lock image. The shadow write of `SIP_DIS` (slot 3), `SYS_DIS` (slot 4), `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` (slot 21) and `REQUIRED_ALGS` (slot 22) on a write-locked field; the unlocked write of the same fields is a control. The three exempt registers with every assigned lock bit burned. The order of a shadow write against the lock on slots 3, 4 and 22, with a zero write to `LOCKS`. A chain of one to four unlocked program rounds over lock slots 1 to 40 with a resense after each, and a version round on `BL1_VERSION` or `BL2_VERSION` with the burned index at 0, 255 or a middle value. The test extends `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test` (unlocked and write-locked cells on `SPARE0` to `SPARE8`), `sep_locked_field_access_irq_path_test` (a write-lock and a read-lock on two spare fields), `sep_efuse_km_public_id_test` (the shadow read of a read-locked field against its unlocked twin), `sep_efuse_image_test` (ten random bits and a resense), `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` (the read-back flag) and `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` (the unlocked shadow write of `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `REQUIRED_ALGS` and `REQUIRED_SIGNERS`: the OR-merge or overwrite, and the write of 0). This test grades only the new cells: the lock bit positions of slots 1 to 40, the unlocked neighbour of each, the sense that ignores locks, the three exempt registers with every assigned lock bit burned, the write-locked shadow write of the four fields and its order against the lock (the unlocked write is a control, and the open owner keeps its grading), the programs on lock slots 1 to 40, the OR over a chain of rounds and the version field storage. The open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: Phase 1 groups `sep_efuse_program_lock_cg` and `sep_efuse_sense_cg` (`cp_episode`, the re-sense bin, filled by the resense after each program round); `sep_efuse_lock_policy_cg` (every coverpoint except `x_lc_prog_gate`; every coverpoint is seed-dependent, one leaf kind per leaf, and closes over a merge of regressions) and `sep_efuse_field_value_cg` (`x_version_bit`, seed-dependent, from the drawn chain leaves).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the leaf kind and the values of the Randomization field of that kind from the seed: the lock layout, the target slot and the program slots, the bit positions and the shadow-write bits; or the single-bit slot and kind; or the order slot and permutation; or the chain round count, version field, index class, fields, bits and read-back flags. Print `SEED seed=<n> kind=<kind>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. A bit address is 8 times the shadow byte offset of the field plus the bit index. W is a multiple of the sense time that the leaf measures at its first sense (the multiple is a test parameter). "Program bit b of f" writes `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_program_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, because nothing shows that a busy bit has risen before the first poll, then reads `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, requires `program_busy` 0 and logs `program_done`, then writes `program_enable` = 0. "Read bit b of f" writes `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 1, `efuse_addr` and `efuse_read_go` in one write, waits the full window W with no early exit, then reads `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, requires `read_busy` 0 and logs `read_done`, then writes `read_enable` = 0. After a refused request, write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`.
. Lock leg. Stage image A: TEST_DEV; every 256-bit field at its fixed vector; narrow fields other than `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at non-zero staged values that have at least one 0 in their used bits; `SBOOT_DIS` and `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0; the drawn lock layout (the target slot locked for write and read, its two neighbours unlocked, slots 1 and 2 unlocked, every other data slot of 3 to 40 at its drawn lock kind); a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits. Release the reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time.
. For each data slot of 1 to 40, in a drawn order, run the steps 3 to 6 in this order, with the step 5 only for a program slot (the target slot, its neighbours slot - 1 and slot + 1 where they are data fields, so not slot 0 and not slot 41, and the three drawn program slots), because each program waits the full window W. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged words, locked or not.
. Read the field through the frontdoor, word by word. Where the field is not read-locked, expect each word to equal the staged word. Where the field is read-locked, log the response and the data of each word as `OBS-LOCK-READ seed=<n> slot=<s> word=<k> resp=<code> rdata=0x<w>` and do not grade them. For a read-locked field, and for each unlocked neighbour of the target slot, read a drawn bit of the used range of the field through the read interface. Where the field is read-locked, this is an error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go on the field. Kind: a request that a read-lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1; the read data and the other flags are not graded. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1. Where the field is not read-locked, expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and `efuse_req_error` = 0.
. Program a zero bit of the used range of the field. Where the field is write-locked, this is an error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go on the field. Kind: a request that a write-lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1 and no change in the bank. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1 and the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` to be none, with no program command at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from the go to the end of W. Where the field is not write-locked, expect `efuse_req_error` = 0, `program_done` = 1 and exactly that bit in the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`, with one program command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within W.
. Where the field is `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` or `REQUIRED_ALGS`: write through the frontdoor the staged value with one bit that the shadow lacks. For `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` the staged value has at least one of bits 1:0 at 0. Where the field is write-locked, this is an error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of the field. Kind: a write that a write-lock blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged value. Where the field is not write-locked, the write is a control of the shadow write path, and an open test grades its OR-merge. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged value OR the new bit.
. Single-bit fields (the drawn slot, 1 or 2, with the drawn kind). Read-lock image: stage image A with the used bit of the slot staged at 1 and a burned read-lock on the slot only. Write-lock image: stage image A with the used bit of the slot staged at 0 and a burned write-lock on the slot only. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time. For a read-lock image run the steps 3 and 4 on the slot, with the frontdoor read and the read-interface request of an unlocked neighbour as the twin. For a write-lock image run the steps 3 and 5 on the slot, with the program of an unlocked neighbour as the twin. No unused bit is programmed.
. Exempt cell. Stage image B: TEST_DEV; `LOCKS` all ones (64 bits); `LOCKS_SPARE` bits 17:0 at 1 and bits 31:18 at 0; `CLASS_KEY` (slot 7) at a fixed vector with no zero word. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` through the frontdoor. Expect the staged values. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` go on a drawn bit of `CLASS_KEY`, which has its read-lock burned in image B. Kind: a request that a read-lock blocks. Expected response: `efuse_req_error` = 1. Read the bit through the read interface. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 1. Write 1 to the three clear bits of `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`. Twin: read a drawn bit of `LOCKS`, which has no lock slot, through the read interface. Expect `read_done` = 1, `read_status` = 0 and `efuse_req_error` = 0. Read the eight words of `CLASS_KEY` through the frontdoor and log the response and the data of each word as `OBS-LOCK-READ`; they are not graded. This is the control of the exempt reads.
. Order cell. Stage image C: TEST_DEV, no lock bit burned, the fields at their fixed vectors, and `REQUIRED_ALGS` at a non-zero staged value with at least three of its six used bits (bits 1:0 of each nibble) at 0, so that the bits a, b and c of the next two steps exist. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Control write: write to the field of a second slot of {3, 4, 22}, which the cell does not lock, the staged value with one bit added. Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged value OR the new bit.
. For the drawn slot s (3, 4 or 22), build the value A as the staged shadow value of the field with a bit a added and the value B as the staged shadow value with a bit b added (a and b differ and are absent from the staged value). Run the three actions in the drawn permutation: write A to the field, write `LOCKS` with the write-lock bit 2s set, write B to the field. Each write of the field that follows the lock in the permutation is an error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of the field. Kind: a write that a write-lock blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. After each action read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and `LOCKS` through the frontdoor.
. Error injection. Point: frontdoor write of 0 to `LOCKS`. Kind: a clear of a set-only register. Expected response: the write-lock bit stays set. Write 0 to `LOCKS`. Read `LOCKS`. Expect the write-lock bit still set. Error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of the field. Kind: a write that the write-lock blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. Write the staged value with a third bit c added to the field (c absent from the staged value, from a and from b). Read the field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect no change.
. Chain leg. Stage image D: TEST_DEV; every lock bit at 0; the version field of the version round at 0 (an exception to the image rule, so that the programmed flag is the only bit set after resense); every other field at its fixed vector or a drawn non-zero narrow value. Preload. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Measure the sense time. The reference is the model image: the staged image D. Read the 256 shadow words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and compare them with the reference (a mismatch fails CHK-PROG-ROUND).
. For each round k of the chain: take a drawn field of lock slots 1 to 40 (not `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE` and not the version field) and a drawn bit of its used range that is 0 in the image. Program the bit with the drawn read-back flag. Expect `efuse_req_error` = 0 and the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` to be exactly that bit. Read the word of the field through the frontdoor. Expect the value before the program. Log `program_status`.
. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read the 256 shadow words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the reference with the bit of the round added in its word and no other change. Read the word of the field through the frontdoor. Expect the old value OR the bit. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the profile row of the new image. The reference of the next round is the model: the reference of this round OR the bit of this round, never a shadow read-back.
. Version round. Program the bit at the drawn index of the version field (index 0, index 255, or the drawn middle index). Expect `efuse_req_error` = 0 and the bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o` to be exactly that bit. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read the eight words of the field. Expect only the programmed flag set.
. Cold reset again. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to rise. Read the eight words of the field. Expect the same value.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-LOCK-CELL
|For each slot that the lock leg or the single-bit image reads, the sensed-shadow output equals the staged value (the sense ignores locks); the frontdoor read equals the staged value when the field is not read-locked; a read-interface request on a read-locked field sets `efuse_req_error`, and the same request on an unlocked neighbour of the target slot ends with `read_done` 1, `read_status` 0 and `efuse_req_error` 0 (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control: a read-lock blocks software reads of the fuse field and its shadow; `efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, register `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`: the eFuse was blocked because of a read or a program lock); a program on a write-locked field sets `efuse_req_error` and changes no bank bit (diff none at `otp_bank_probe_o`), and a program on an unlocked field leaves `efuse_req_error` at 0, ends with `program_done` 1 and lands exactly the programmed bit at `otp_bank_probe_o` (`efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, register `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, field `efuse_req_error`: the request was blocked by a program lock); a shadow write is blocked when the field is write-locked and gives the OR of the staged value and the new bit when not, a control whose OR-merge an open test grades (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, sections Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control and Fuse Fields). A DUT that places a lock bit one slot off, locks a neighbour or blocks the sense fails. Anchor: the sensed-shadow output, a frontdoor readback, the `efuse_req_error` flag and the read-interface status, the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`, and the post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Control: the target slot has an unblocked twin on each of its unlocked neighbours in the same leaf, and every other blocked cell has an unblocked cell of the same operation on another slot of the same leaf. The specification does not state the response or the data of a frontdoor shadow read that a read-lock blocks, so the leaf logs them as `OBS-LOCK-READ` and does not grade them. The check also grades that no program command reaches the post-guard port (`efuse_shim_command_req_o`) from the go to the end of W for a program on a write-locked field. Control on the same tap: a program on an unlocked field of the same leaf shows one program command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within W; a missing command fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-CELL PASS seed=<n> slot=<s> field=<name> wl=<0\|1> rl=<0\|1> staged=0x<w> sense_probe=0x<w> read=<0x<w>\|logged> rd_if=<blocked\|accepted\|na> rd_req_err=<0\|1\|na> prog=<landed\|blocked> req_err=<0\|1> otp_diff=<a\|none\|na> shadow_wr=<or\|blocked\|na> shim_cmd=<0\|1\|na>`.
|CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT
|With every assigned lock bit burned, `LC_STATE`, `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` read their staged values through the frontdoor (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control: `LOCKS` and `LOCKS_SPARE` have no lock slot, and `LC_STATE` ignores its assigned lock bits). A DUT that read-locks `LC_STATE` or lets `LOCKS` lock itself fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback. Control: image B has every assigned write-lock and read-lock bit burned, shown by the `LOCKS` read, and a read-interface request on `CLASS_KEY`, which has its read-lock burned in image B, sets `efuse_req_error`, while the same request on `LOCKS`, which has no lock slot, ends with `read_done` 1 and `efuse_req_error` 0, so a DUT that ignores read-locks cannot pass the control. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-EXEMPT PASS seed=<n> target=<LC_STATE\|LOCKS\|LOCKS_SPARE> exp=0x<v> got=0x<v> ctl_field=CLASS_KEY ctl_req_err=1 ctl_twin=LOCKS ctl_twin_done=1 ctl_twin_req_err=0`, and `OBS-LOCK-READ seed=<n> slot=7 word=<k> resp=<code> rdata=0x<w>`.
|CHK-LOCK-ORDER
|The field shadow equals the golden for the drawn permutation: a write lands only before the lock, the lock bit stays set after a write of 0 to `LOCKS`, and a later write of a third bit leaves the shadow unchanged (`sep_efuse_map.adoc`, registers `LOCKS`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`). A DUT that lets a write land after the lock or clears the lock on a zero write fails. Anchor: the sensed-shadow output and a frontdoor readback of `LOCKS`. Control: the control write of the same image lands on the field of a second slot, so the shadow write path is alive; where a write precedes the lock, it lands; and the third bit is absent from the shadow before the third-bit write, so the write would add a bit if the lock were clear. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-ORDER PASS seed=<n> field=<name> perm=<order> shadow=0x<v> exp=0x<v> lock_after_zero=<1> third_bit_landed=0`.
|CHK-PROG-ROUND
|After a program the shadow word of the field (frontdoor readback) is unchanged, and the OTP image at `otp_bank_probe_o` equals the previous image plus exactly the programmed bit. After the resense the shadow word equals the old value OR the bit, no other of the 256 shadow words changes, and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the profile row of the new image (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`, sections Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile and Combining Rule: OR then Invert). A DUT whose shadow follows before the sense or that clears an earlier bit fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback, the sensed-shadow output and the OTP bank diff at `otp_bank_probe_o`. The reference is the model image (the staged image D plus the bits programmed in earlier rounds), not a shadow read-back. Control: the first compare after the first sense matches the staged image, and each round compares all 256 words with the model, so an earlier bit that clears shows at the next compare. After the resense the frontdoor read of the field word equals the old value OR the bit, so the same frontdoor read that stays unchanged after the program can show the bit; a frontdoor word without the bit fails the check. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-ROUND PASS seed=<n> round=<k> field=<name> bit_addr=<a> rb=<0\|1> otp_diff=<a\|na> shadow_pre=0x<w> shadow_after_prog=0x<w> shadow_after_sense=0x<w> fd_after_sense=0x<w>`.
|CHK-PROG-VERSION
|After the resense only the programmed flag is set in `BL1_VERSION` or `BL2_VERSION`, and it stays set after a second resense (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Anti-Rollback Security-Version Flags). A DUT that sets a lower or higher flag, or loses the flag, fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of the eight words of the field. Control: the field is staged at 0, so a second set bit shows. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-VERSION PASS seed=<n> field=<BL1\|BL2> bit=<i> word_after_sense=0x<w> word_after_resense2=0x<w>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The test does not write `LC_STATE`, and the program of its gated bits belongs to `sep_efuse_transient_rma_rand_test`. It does not draw the kind read alone on slots 11, 13 and 15, and it does not grade the response or the data of a frontdoor shadow read or a read-interface read that a read-lock blocks. It does not grade the `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` write class, because the shadow write data is the staged value with one added bit. The unlocked shadow write of the four fields is a control here. The open tests `sep_efuse_program_lock_matrix_test`, `sep_locked_field_access_irq_path_test`, `sep_efuse_km_public_id_test`, `sep_efuse_image_test`, `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` and `sep_efuse_set_only_monotonicity_test` keep the cells that they grade.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 6.27 `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense (no `+skip_fuse_sense`), 4 seeds; one testlist entry; the seed draws the state of the t=0 image (the preload hook stages it from the seed); tool: Verilator and VCS; testlist `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`, groups `all` and `cpu_stub`. The JTAG master is live in this target.

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_secure_tm_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor (`ocah_axi_vip` on `s_axi`, CPU-LSU master of `lsu_stub_all_live`), including `FEAT_CTRL`. JTAG master on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o` (`ocah_axi_vip` AXI-Lite master). Ports and probes, approved: `secure_tm_req_i`, `secure_tm_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `security_disable_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o` (the masked sensed-shadow output). The OTP bank model with its image preload, and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface in the strap-0 epoch (the strap-1 epoch blocks it). No inject. The post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the command statements of CHK-STM-PROG and CHK-STM-PROG-CTL) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). The seed draws the state {TEST_DEV, PROD, RMA_SIP} with the weights 2, 1 and 1, because only TEST_DEV runs the JTAG leg. Every leaf writes all four targets {an unset `LOCKS` bit, an unset assigned `LOCKS_SPARE` bit (17:0), a `SIP_DIS` bit, a `SYS_DIS` bit}. Dimensions: the target order and the bit of each target; the field and the bit of each of the five program classes; the written bits of both write sources. Trap filters: the strap is stable during an epoch and changes only while the cold reset is asserted; each written bit is absent from the shadow at its write, and the frontdoor bit and the JTAG bit of a target differ; no target bit locks a slot that another target, a program target or a secret field uses; the secret fields are non-zero; programs burn only in the strap-0 epoch; no step writes `LC_STATE`. `reseed = 4` gives four states per regression. The state is a seed-dependent cell: the three states close in about 2 regressions, and a regression runs the JTAG leg with a probability of about 0.94 (1 - (1/2)^4). The strap order is the same on every seed, and every leaf runs both epochs, the frontdoor targets, the five program classes and the secret cells.

*Objective*: With the TEST_EN strap (`secure_tm_req_i`) high at sense completion, `secure_tm_o` is 1 until cold reset clears it. While it is 1 no program changes the bank for any field, the four secret fields read zero at the sensed-shadow output while the stored value and the register read stay, and `FEAT_CTRL` equals its value at strap 0. The protection shadows `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` ignore software writes from the frontdoor, which is a control of the other cells because an open test grades it, and ignore writes from the JTAG master (a new cell). No step writes `LC_STATE` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc` (sections Test Mode Entry, Entry and Exit, Protection While Active); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (sections OTP Fuse Controller, Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection); `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Overrides and Final Gates (SEC_DIS), `secure_tm` and the Secure Scan Chain); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Secure Test Mode (secure_tm_i), for the command block only; its list of masked fields is not used, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc` (ports `secure_tm_o`, `secure_tm_req_i`). The secret-field cells grade the four fields that `otp_fuse_controller.adoc` and `test_mode.adoc` name, at the sensed-shadow output. The view of the masked fields at the Key Manager consumer belongs to `sep_km_otp_interface_rand_test`. Both chapters that list the blocked sources name JTAG (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: software, JTAG and TRoot; `lifecycle_controller.adoc`: SEP, OTP, JTAG and TRoot), so the test grades the software source and the JTAG source. TRoot is not defined, so the test does not drive it (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The meaning of the reset-only exit is not stated, so the test holds the strap stable. The JTAG cells run in TEST_DEV: in PROD and RMA_SIP the lifecycle gate refuses every JTAG shadow write (`otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control), so a refusal under strap 1 and a control under strap 0 cannot differ there. The specification states the JTAG restriction of PROD and RMA_SIP as one that applies "in addition to" the shadow-register access control of the field table, so the strap-0 JTAG write is the control of each strap-1 JTAG cell, and a cell whose control does not land is logged and not counted.

*Test Coverage*: A strap-1 epoch and a strap-0 epoch in each of TEST_DEV, PROD and RMA_SIP. The latch of `secure_tm_o` before and after sense done. Frontdoor shadow writes of the four targets, as a control. JTAG shadow writes of the same targets in TEST_DEV, strap 1 against the strap-0 control. Programs of a zero bit in each of five classes (a 256-bit field other than the two token-digest fields, a 32-bit field, a disable bit, a `LOCKS` bit, a spare word outside the five protection shadows). The four secret fields at the sensed-shadow output and at the frontdoor. A SEC_DIS match and a mismatch at the end of the strap-0 epoch. The test extends `sep_efuse_secure_tm_write_lock_test` (writes `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`, `LC_STATE`, `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` with the strap low and high and checks that they do not move) and `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` (the strap-following of `secure_tm_o`, the secret blanking and the program block under secure test mode). This test grades only the new cells: the five program classes at the OTP bank (by the direct read of the strap-0 epoch), the added clauses of the secret check, and the JTAG source of the protection shadow writes. The secret check is a stated superset of the secret blanking of `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test`, which grades that the strap-1 value at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` differs from the staged value; the added clauses are the exact zero at the probe, the staged value at the frontdoor read and the staged value in the OTP word. The strap latch, the frontdoor shadow write block and the SEC_DIS result are controls here, and the open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_secure_tm_cg` (`x_secret_probe` (strap-1 exact zero), `x_secret_read_kept` and `x_prog_block`; the JTAG cells credit no bin, see `SEP_FCOV.adoc`, Bins not graded of the eFuse section); Phase 1 group `sep_feat_ctrl_cg`. The SEC_DIS leg and the `FEAT_CTRL` reads at each strap are controls (CHK-STM-CTL) and credit no Phase 2 bin.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the state, the target order and target bits, the program targets and the written bits from the seed. Print `SEED seed=<n> state=<name>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds. "Program bit b of f" writes `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` (8 times the shadow byte offset of f plus b) and `efuse_program_go` in one write, waits for the window `W_prog`, then writes `program_enable` = 0. `W_prog` is a bounded wait of a multiple of the sense time that the leaf measures at its first sense; the strap-1 epoch and the strap-0 epoch use the same `W_prog`.
. Stage the OTP image: the drawn state in `LC_STATE`; the four secret fields `CHIPLET_UID`, `SIP_UID`, `SYS_UID` and `CLASS_KEY` at their fixed non-zero vectors; every other 256-bit field at its fixed vector; no lock bit burned; a disable-vector pair that the vector filter admits. Run `CHK-BIND`.
. Strap-1 epoch. Set `secure_tm_req_i` = 1 while the cold reset is asserted and hold it for the epoch. Release the reset. Sample `secure_tm_o` on every cycle from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises. Expect 0. After sense done poll `secure_tm_o` until it is high. Expect 1.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` as the baseline of the epoch. Expect the profile row of the state and the staged pair.
. Frontdoor shadow writes. Error injection. Point: frontdoor shadow write of each of the four targets at strap 1. Kind: a write that secure test mode blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. For each of the four targets write the shadow value with one bit that the shadow lacks. Read the target through the frontdoor. Expect the value before the write. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect the baseline.
. JTAG leg, in TEST_DEV only. Error injection. Point: JTAG shadow write of each of the four targets at strap 1. Kind: a write that secure test mode blocks. Expected response: no change in the shadow. For each of the four targets write through the JTAG master the shadow value with one other bit that the shadow lacks. Read the target through the frontdoor. Expect the value before the write. The response code is not graded.
. Programs. Error injection. Point: `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` go at strap 1. Kind: a program that secure test mode blocks. Expected response: no change in the bank; the status value is not graded. For each of the five classes, program a zero bit of the drawn field, with the drawn bit. The direct read is blocked at strap 1, so the bank bits are read in the strap-0 epoch (step 11).
. Secret cell. Read the eight words of each of the four secret fields at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect 0. Read the same fields through the frontdoor. Expect the staged words.
. Strap-0 epoch. Assert the cold reset. Set `secure_tm_req_i` = 0 while the reset is asserted and hold it for the epoch. Release the reset. Sample `secure_tm_o` from reset release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises. Expect 0. After sense done expect `secure_tm_o` = 0. Read `FEAT_CTRL` as the baseline of this epoch. Expect the baseline of the strap-1 epoch.
. Repeat the writes of the steps 5 and 6 with the strap at 0, using another absent bit for each source. Read each target. Expect the value OR the new bit. Where a JTAG write does not land, log `CHK-STM-JTAG N/A` for that target. In a TEST_DEV leaf at least one JTAG write lands; if none lands, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Bank read. Before any program of this epoch, read through the direct read the word of each of the five targets of step 7 and the first word of each secret field. Expect each of the five target bits at 0, so that no strap-1 program of step 7 landed, and each secret word equal to its staged word. Program the same five bits as in step 7, each with the same window `W_prog`. Read the five target words through the direct read. Expect each of the five bits at 1.
. Read the eight words of each secret field at `efuse_shadow_probe_o`. Expect the staged words (the control of the probe).
. SEC_DIS leg. Write `TOKEN_ZERO` to the SEC_DIS token array and go on SEC_DIS. Poll the SEC_DIS match register. Expect `0x15`. Expect `security_disable_o` = 1. This is the control of the revision-enable assumption: a leaf in which the probe stays low fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Read `FEAT_CTRL`. Expect all ones. Write `TOKEN_OTHER`, go and poll. Expect `0x2a` and `security_disable_o` = 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BIND
|The SHA-256 of 32 zero bytes, read as the 256-bit value of FIPS 180-4, equals the SEC_DIS digest that the bench binds (`security_disable.adoc`, section Implementation Requirements). A wrong binding makes `TOKEN_ZERO` a mismatch token, so the SEC_DIS leg could not activate. Anchor: the bound digest constant at the bench against the golden digest. Control: the SEC_DIS match of the SEC_DIS leg raises `security_disable_o`, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-BIND PASS seed=<n> digest=0x<h>`.
|CHK-STM-CTL
|Controls of the other checkers, graded by open tests and not claimed as new contract. `secure_tm_o` is 0 from reset release until sense done and equals the strap after it (`test_mode.adoc`, section Entry and Exit). At strap 1 each target shadow stays unchanged and `FEAT_CTRL` equals the strap-0 baseline and the profile row (`test_mode.adoc`: `SECURE_TM` does not qualify the feature bits). At strap 0 each frontdoor write lands. In the strap-0 epoch the SEC_DIS match gives `security_disable_o` = 1 and an all-ones `FEAT_CTRL` (`security_disable.adoc`, section Token Activation). A control that fails fails the leaf. Anchor: the ports `secure_tm_o` and `security_disable_o`, and frontdoor readback. Evidence: `CHK-STM-CTL PASS seed=<n> strap=<0\|1> secure_tm_before_sense=0 secure_tm_after_sense=<0\|1> target=<name> landed=<0\|1> feat=0x<64b> [secdis_feat=0xffffffffffffffff]`.
|CHK-STM-PROG
|At strap 1 none of the five program gos issued after sense done changes its bank bit: in the strap-0 epoch the direct read of the word of each target, taken before any program of that epoch, reads the bit at 0 (`test_mode.adoc`, section Protection While Active: the fuse write-enable inputs stay inactive; `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register Updates and Test-Mode Protection). A DUT that lets a program through under secure test mode fails. Anchor: the direct read of the target word in the strap-0 epoch. Control: CHK-STM-PROG-CTL, which shows the same programs land inside the same window `W_prog` at strap 0, read by the same direct read, and show their commands at `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. The check also grades that no `PROGRAM` or `PROGRAM_READ_BACK` command reaches the post-guard port at strap 1 (anchor: `efuse_shim_command_req_o`). Evidence: `CHK-STM-PROG PASS seed=<n> strap=1 field=<name> bit_read=0`.
|CHK-STM-PROG-CTL
|At strap 0 each of the five programs lands in the bank, the spare word included, inside the same window `W_prog`, shown by the direct read. A leaf in which a program does not land fails, because the window of CHK-STM-PROG would not prove that no program runs. Each strap-0 program also shows one `PROGRAM` or `PROGRAM_READ_BACK` command with its address at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` within `W_prog`; a missing command fails the leaf, because the tap statement of CHK-STM-PROG would not prove that no command runs. Anchor: the direct read of the target word and `efuse_shim_command_req_o`. Evidence: `CHK-STM-PROG-CTL PASS seed=<n> strap=0 field=<name> bit_read=1 shim_cmd=1`.
|CHK-STM-SECRET
|At strap 1 the four secret fields read zero at the sensed-shadow output, while the frontdoor read and the OTP bank keep the staged value (`test_mode.adoc`, section Protection While Active: the controller masks the secret shadow outputs to zero, masking does not erase the stored value, and register reads keep their access controls). This check is a stated superset of the secret blanking of `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` (strap-1 probe value differs from the staged value); it claims only the exact zero at the probe, the kept frontdoor read and the kept OTP word. A DUT that masks to a non-zero value or erases the stored value fails. Anchor: `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, a frontdoor readback and, in the strap-0 epoch, the direct read of the first word of the field. Control: the strap-0 epoch shows the staged secret on the same probe, and a secret field that is zero at the probe in the strap-0 epoch fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-STM-SECRET PASS seed=<n> strap=1 field=<name> probe=0x0 ctl_probe=0x<staged> reg_read_eq_staged=1 otp_word0_eq_staged=1`, printed after the strap-0 epoch of the same field.
|CHK-STM-JTAG
|In TEST_DEV at strap 1, a JTAG write of one new bit to each drawn protection shadow leaves the shadow equal to its value before the write (`test_mode.adoc` and `otp_fuse_controller.adoc`: updates from every source are blocked, JTAG named). A DUT that lets the JTAG source update a protection shadow under secure test mode fails. Anchor: frontdoor readback of the shadow. Control: the strap-0 JTAG write to the same target lands as the OR of the old value and the bit. A cell whose control does not land logs `CHK-STM-JTAG N/A` and does not count. A TEST_DEV leaf requires at least one `CHK-STM-JTAG PASS`; a TEST_DEV leaf with none fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`, because a JTAG write path that never lands would otherwise pass with N/A on every target. In PROD and RMA_SIP the leg does not run and the leaf logs `CHK-STM-JTAG N/A seed=<n> state=<name> reason=lifecycle_gate`; that line is the required output of the checker in those leaves. Evidence: `CHK-STM-JTAG PASS seed=<n> target=<name> strap=1 before=0x<w> after=0x<w> ctl_after=0x<w>`, or `CHK-STM-JTAG N/A seed=<n> target=<name> ctl_landed=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every checker that the leaf requires PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0. Every leaf requires `CHK-BIND`, `CHK-STM-CTL`, `CHK-STM-PROG`, `CHK-STM-PROG-CTL` and `CHK-STM-SECRET`. A TEST_DEV leaf also requires at least one `CHK-STM-JTAG PASS`; a PROD or RMA_SIP leaf requires the `CHK-STM-JTAG N/A` line with `reason=lifecycle_gate` and no PASS. A `CHK-STM-JTAG N/A` line for a target whose strap-0 control does not land is not a miss while another target of the leaf has a PASS.

*Scope limit*: The test drives no TRoot source, writes no `LC_STATE`, holds the strap stable, and makes no SEC_DIS go with the strap at 1. It does not grade the exit of secure test mode other than the cold reset between the epochs, the status value of a blocked program, or the response code of a blocked write. It does not grade masking of fields other than the four named secret fields. The JTAG cells run in TEST_DEV only. The open tests `sep_efuse_secure_tm_write_lock_test` and `sep_efuse_lcc_lc_state_stitch_test` keep the cells that they grade, and the Key Manager use of the masked fields belongs to the Key Manager tests.

'''''
[[sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test]]
===== 6.28 `sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense, 4 seeds; one testlist entry; the seed draws the state of the t=0 image (the preload hook stages it from the seed); tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_jtag_access_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: JTAG master on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_i` and `axil_sep_otp_jtag_resp_o` (approved). Frontdoor master, the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live`, with the response code of every access (approved). OTP image preload (approved), and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface (the read interface is not the unit under test; approved). `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and `secure_tm_o` (approved). A SHA-256 model that builds the golden match code from the array content at each go. The fixed vectors `TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. The post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the command statement of CHK-JTAG) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RANDCFG). The seed draws the state, PROD or RMA_SIP with equal weight. The access list of every leaf holds each of the eight (class, operation) cells at least once: shadow word read and write, interface-control register read and write, token word read and write, `TOKEN_EOP` write and match register read; the seed draws the order of these eight accesses and the other N - 8 accesses. Dimensions: access count N (15 to 20; the eight cells need at least 15 accesses, because the `TOKEN_EOP` write follows the eight word writes of its array, the token word read follows one of those writes, and the match read and the four denied cells take one access each), per drawn access the operation, the class, the in-class address and the written pattern, the two free spare bits, the `SYS_DIS` and `SIP_DIS` function-group bits, and the vector (`TOKEN_T1`, `TOKEN_T2` or `TOKEN_OTHER`) that each token-word write takes its word from. A draw never makes a token value. Trap filters: no `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` access; no concurrent frontdoor and JTAG traffic; no token word read before the leaf writes that word; a `TOKEN_EOP` write carries one go bit, on the SIP or the chiplet channel, and follows the write of all eight words of that array; no word write between a go and its result; a go gives a golden result that differs from the previous result of that channel (Match after Mismatch, Mismatch after Match), so a stale read cannot equal it, and the word writes between two goes on a channel rebuild the array so that the SHA-256 model gives the other result (the draw is checked against the model and a sequence that fails is redrawn); the token-block accesses run in the drawn order, interleaved by kind; the SEC_DIS array holds `TOKEN_OTHER` only and gets no go; a match register is only read and `TOKEN_EOP` is only written; a shadow-word write draws only `LOCKS`, `LOCKS_SPARE`, `SIP_DIS`, `SYS_DIS`, `REQUIRED_SIGNERS` or `REQUIRED_ALGS` (the shadow registers with writable bits in `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`), only a register that has a writable bit at 0 in the staged image, and a pattern that sets such a bit, while every other shadow word is drawn as a read only; the drawn `SIP_DIS` and `SYS_DIS` function-group bits leave at least one writable bit at 0 in each. `reseed = 4` gives four access sequences per regression. The state is a seed-dependent cell: a regression runs both states with a probability of about 0.88 (1 - 2 x (1/2)^4), and a merge of regressions closes the 14 graded state-class-operation cells (the PROD token word read and write are logged, see Test Coverage).

*Objective*: In PROD and in RMA_SIP the JTAG port reaches only the token block (three token arrays, `TOKEN_EOP`, three match registers). Every other JTAG read or write completes with an error response and changes nothing. No feature-control bit gates the port. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Lifecycle State (LC_STATE) Effects); `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc` (sections Debug and Test Port Path Gating, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path; Per-LC-state Feature Control Profile); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_mmr.adoc` (section eFuse Token Registers Address Map); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`. The specification does not state the JTAG policy in the other states or before sense, whether `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT` belongs to the token block, the response code of a denied access, or the arbitration of concurrent AXI and JTAG requests. The test draws only PROD and RMA_SIP, serializes the two ports and grades a response that is not OKAY. The integrity-error state is a leg of `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`, which is blocked: it needs its injection point.

*Test Coverage*: Two states (PROD, RMA_SIP) by five target classes: shadow word (read and write), interface-control register (read and write), token word (read and write), `TOKEN_EOP` (write only) and match register (read only), which gives 16 state-class-operation cells, of which this test grades 14. The token match through the JTAG port with every DBG_1 and DBG_2 feature bit closed, in both states. The target readback after each denied access and the OTP bit p at the end of the leaf. The open test `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test` grades the response of the PROD read of the shadow map, of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and of the token-block sample (`SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]`, `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH`), and the PROD token word write and readback (OKAY, and the word last written). The open test `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test` grades the shadow map and `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` in TEST_DEV. They keep those cells, and this test logs the response of those PROD accesses (and the data of a PROD token word read) and does not grade it. Only the new cells are graded here: the write of every class other than the token word in PROD, the write of every class in RMA_SIP, the read of every class in RMA_SIP, the token word readback in RMA_SIP and the match golden of the channels with a go, the unchanged target after each denied access and the unburned bit p at the end of the leaf, in both states (including the PROD denied reads), and the token match with every debug bit closed in both states. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_jtag_policy_cg` (`cp_state` for PROD and RMA_SIP, `cp_class`, `cp_resp`, `x_state_class_op`, `cp_gate_independent_of_feat`; `cp_state`, `x_state_class_op` and `cp_gate_independent_of_feat` are seed-dependent, because one leaf draws one state).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw from the seed the state, N, the order of the eight required (class, operation) accesses among the N, and for each access the operation, class, in-class address and pattern. Print `SEED seed=<n> state=<name>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Draw the control bit c and the probe bit p: two different spare bits that read 0 in the image and whose field is unlocked. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Stage the OTP image through the preload: the `LC_STATE` encoding of the drawn state; `SIP_DIS` with every DBG_1 and DBG_2 bit set (an exception to the vector filter: the test grades no demotion, owner veto or RMA bypass); `SYS_DIS` with a drawn non-zero value that has at least one 0 bit; `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T1` and `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_DIGEST` as the digest of `TOKEN_T2`, staged by the digest rule of this bucket; `TRANSIENT_RMA_EN` at 0, so that no SIP or chiplet Match moves the state shadow and `FEAT_CTRL` stays at the profile of the drawn state; every lock bit 0; the other fields by the image rules.
. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0. Cold reset and wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`. Measure the sense time; the window W is a multiple of it (the multiple is a test parameter). Read `secure_tm_o`; it reads 0, so the shadow protection of secure test mode cannot hide an accepted write.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Both read the same value, and bits 47:0 (DBG_1 and DBG_2) read 0. If a debug bit reads 1, the leaf fails with `CHK-JTAG-TOKEN FAIL`.
. Set a status flag that a denied write could clear. Through the frontdoor, write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 1, `efuse_addr` = 8192 (the first bit address past the 256 words of 32 bits) and `efuse_read_go` = 1. Wait the full window W with no early exit; `read_done` is not graded, because the register description does not state that an out-of-range read sets it. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`; `efuse_read_addr_error` reads 1, else the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Write `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` with `read_enable` = 0.
. Control program. Through the frontdoor, write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of c and `efuse_program_go` = 1. Wait the full window W with no early exit. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`; `program_done` reads 1. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with all fields 0. Read the word of c through the direct read; bit c reads 1, and bit p reads 0 in the word of p.
. For each drawn access of a denied class (shadow word or interface-control register), in the drawn order: read the target through the frontdoor; issue the JTAG read or write; record the response code; read the target through the frontdoor again. A shadow-word write pattern sets a writable bit that reads 0 in the staged image, so an accepted write changes the readback. A write to `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` sets bit 10 (`efuse_read_addr_error_clear`). A write to `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` sets `program_enable`, `efuse_data` and `efuse_program_go` with `efuse_addr` = the bit address of p, so an accepted write burns p. A write to `EFUSE_READ_CTRL` sets `read_enable` and `efuse_read_go` with a drawn `efuse_addr` below 8192. A read pattern is the address alone. Error injection: the point is the JTAG master; the kind is a read or write outside the token block; the expected response is a response that is not OKAY and no change. The read data is not graded. After the last denied access of the leaf wait the window W, then read the word of p through the direct read. Bit p reads 0.
. Run the drawn token-block accesses in the drawn order, one at a time, each as the action of its kind in the four steps that follow. The kinds are interleaved as drawn and do not run as separate passes.
. Token-word write: issue the JTAG write of the drawn vector word to the drawn array and word index (index 0 holds `token[31:0]`); record the response; in RMA_SIP it is OKAY, and in PROD it is logged and not graded.
. Token-word read: issue the JTAG read of a word that the leaf has written; record the response and the data; in RMA_SIP the response is OKAY and the data is the word last written, and in PROD both are logged and not graded.
. `TOKEN_EOP` write: issue the JTAG write with one go bit (bit 0 for the SIP array, bit 8 for the chiplet array) after the eight words of that array are written; record the response; it is OKAY. The array content at the go gives a golden that differs from the previous result of that channel. Poll the JTAG read of that channel's match register until it reads the SHA-256 golden of the array content at the go: Match is 0x15, Mismatch is 0x2a. An Idle (0x00) read keeps the poll running.
. Match-register read: issue the JTAG read; record the response. For a channel with a go in the leaf, compare the data with the golden of its last go. For a channel with no go since power-on, log the data in `OBS-JTAG-MATCH-NOGO` and do not grade it.
. Token match under closed debug bits. If the last SIP result is Match, first write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` and go bit 0 and poll `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the JTAG master until it reads 0x2a, so the result that follows cannot be a stale read. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_T1` through the JTAG master to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I[0]` to `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_I[7]`, each answered OKAY. Write 0x1 to `TOKEN_EOP` through the JTAG master. Poll `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the JTAG master until it reads 0x15 (6'b010101). Read `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor; it reads 0x15.
. Read `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Bits 47:0 read 0 in both.
. End the match. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SIP array through the JTAG master and write 0x1 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor until it reads 0x2a. If the chiplet channel holds a Match, do the same on that channel with `TOKEN_OTHER`, go bit 8 and `RMA_CHIPLET_TOKEN_MATCH`. The next leaf starts with no held match.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-JTAG
|Each access answers as its class requires. For the allowed classes the response is OKAY, except the PROD token word read and write and the PROD read of `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH`, whose response (and for a token word read, the data) is logged and graded by `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test`: in RMA_SIP a token word read returns the word last written, and a match read of a channel with a go in the leaf returns the SHA-256 golden result of the last go (`hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, sections Token Matching Mechanism and Token Submission). A match read of a channel with no go since power-on answers OKAY and logs its value without grading it, because the retained digest and its valid bit have no reset value. For the denied classes (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Shadow Register and JTAG Access Control) the response is not OKAY (except the PROD read of a shadow word or of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, whose response is logged and graded by `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test`), the target reads the same at the frontdoor before and after, and the bit p reads 0 at the direct read at the end of the leaf. A shadow-word write changes a writable bit that reads 0, so an accepted write shows in the readback. A DUT that lets a write reach a shadow word or an interface-control register, answers OKAY for a denied access, burns the bit p, or blocks the token block fails. Anchor: the JTAG response code, the frontdoor readback of the target (a real readback) and the direct read of the word of p. Control: the control program of step 6 sets bit c at the same direct read, so a burn can show; the status flag of step 5 reads 1 before the denied write to the status register, so a taken clear shows; the allowed accesses answer OKAY in each state. No program command reaches the post-guard port `efuse_shim_command_req_o` after a denied write; its control is one program command at the tap for the program of step 6, and a leaf in which that command does not show fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: one line per access, `CHK-JTAG PASS seed=<n> state=<PROD\|RMA_SIP> op=<rd\|wr> tgt=<class> addr=0x<a> exp=<allow\|deny> resp=<code> resp_graded=<1\|0> data_ok=<1\|na> target_unchanged=<1\|na>`. For a denied access `target_unchanged` is 1. For an allowed access it is `na`. A logged PROD access prints `resp_graded=0 data_ok=na`, and a logged PROD token word read adds `data=0x<d> last_wr=0x<w>`. One line at the end of the leaf, `CHK-JTAG PASS seed=<n> state=<PROD\|RMA_SIP> tgt=otp_word_p p_bit_read=0 ctl_bit_read=1 ctl_cmd=1`, reports the direct reads.
|CHK-JTAG-TOKEN
|A JTAG write of `TOKEN_T1` into the SIP array and a go at `TOKEN_EOP` bit 0 give the SIP match 0x15 at a frontdoor read, while `FEAT_CTRL` has every DBG_1 and DBG_2 bit at 0 before and after the go (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc`, row SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL path: no feature-control bit gates the path; `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc`, section Token Matching Mechanism). A DUT that gates the token path on a feature-control bit shows a response that is not OKAY or a match value other than 0x15, and fails. The leaf fails if `FEAT_CTRL` has an open debug bit, because the check could then not tell a gated port from an open one. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of `RMA_SIP_TOKEN_MATCH` and of `FEAT_CTRL`, and `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. Control: in the same leaf the allowed accesses of CHK-JTAG answer OKAY while the debug bits are closed. Evidence: `CHK-JTAG-TOKEN PASS seed=<n> state=<PROD\|RMA_SIP> feat=0x<64b> dbg_open=0x0 sip_match=0x15`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The JTAG policy in TEST_DEV, PROD_END, RMA_CHIPLET, INVALID and before sense, the access to `TOKEN_MATCH_FAULT`, the arbitration of concurrent AXI and JTAG requests, the read data of a denied read and the code of a denied response are not claimed (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). No SEC_DIS go is made, so the leaf needs no SEC_DIS digest. The integrity-error state is graded in `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test`. The open test `sep_efuse_jtag_axil_el2_cpu_mux_test` keeps the grade of the response of the PROD read of the shadow map, of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and of the token-block sample, and of the PROD token word write and readback, and the open test `sep_efuse_jtag_window_allow_test` keeps the TEST_DEV cells; this entry logs those responses and grades only the new cells listed in Test Coverage.

'''''

[[sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test]]
===== 6.29 `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense, `+sep_edn_jtag_reset_waive`, 3 seeds; every leaf runs one class, B or C, which the seed draws with equal weight; tool: VCS only, because the reset behaviour and the unreset shadow values need X; testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`

*Source Module*: `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor master, the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live` (approved). OTP image preload (approved). `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `security_disable_o`, `secure_tm_req_i` and `secure_tm_o` (approved boundary ports). `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o` and the SEP reset net (approved read-only probes). IC_RESET TDR driver on the SEP slice of the override (approved). The fixed vectors `TOKEN_ZERO` and `TOKEN_OTHER`. The post-guard command tap `efuse_shim_command_req_o` (the class C statement of CHK-SDIS-HOLD) is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. No inject.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: the class, B or C with equal weight, the match offset k after reset release (class B, between the bounds k_lo and k_hi that the control sense and the match latency L of step 3 give), the staged image (which fields hold non-zero distinct words, at least four, with at least one below byte offset 0x200 and at least one at or above it, so that the staged loads span the sense), the disable-vector pair from the vector filter. Trap filters: one match per simulation segment; every class ends with a mismatch and a cold reset; `secure_tm_req_i` held at 0. `reseed = 3` gives three classes per regression. The class is a seed-dependent cell: a regression runs both classes with a probability of 0.75 (1 - 2 x (1/2)^3), and the cells that only one class fills (the before-sense and during-sense bins of `cp_match_phase` and the two phase bins of `cp_stops_sense`) close over a merge of regressions.

*Objective*: A SEC_DIS match made before sense keeps sense done low and every sensed shadow word at its reset value. A match made during sense keeps sense done low and the sensed shadow words unchanged. A cold reset under the match leaves every shadow word at its reset value with sensing off. After a mismatch hash and a cold reset, a full sense loads the staged image; the sense and the feature control of that recovery are logged controls. The sense stop, the OKAY map read, the feature override and the drop after a mismatch hash are logged controls here, because `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades them. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc` (sections Token Activation, Fuse Sensing and the Shadow Map, Reset Persistence); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the SLVERR of the map window until sense completes); `hw/sys/sep/doc/token_processing.adoc` (sections Token Matching Mechanism and Attack Countermeasures, Token Submission); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/architecture.adoc` (section Security Disable Bypass Mechanism); `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc` (section Isolation and Reset Sequencing); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (reset values). `test_mode.adoc` and `security_disable.adoc` word the strap sample edge at the early capture point differently, so the strap is held low (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The shadow reset value of `LC_STATE` and the upper nibble of `lc_state_o` before sense are not graded (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).

*Test Coverage*: Class B, a `TOKEN_ZERO` match that lands during sense while a staged non-zero shadow word still holds its reset value, then a release of the SEP reset through the IC_RESET TDR. Class C, a match held over a cold reset with the token arrays retained, so the match is present from reset release and before sense. In both classes: the sensed shadow words held over the window, a cold reset under the match, the reset value of every shadow field after it, and the recovery by a mismatch hash, a cold reset and a full sense, which is a logged control. Logged controls: sense done low and `security_disable_o` at 1 over the window, an OKAY shadow-map read, the sense-done time, the shadow words and the `FEAT_CTRL` value of the recovery sense, a control sense that measures the sense time, the SLVERR of a map read before sense done in that sense, and the staged non-zero fields reading non-zero there. `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades the match override of `FEAT_CTRL`, the drop after a mismatch, the stop of sensing, the OKAY read of the map while sensing is open, the full sense after a mismatch and a reset together with the `FEAT_CTRL` golden of the staged vector pair after it, and the match after sense. `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test` grades the reset override. The open owners keep those cells. Only the new cells are graded here: the class C shadow words at their reset values over the window, the class B unchanged sensed shadow words, and the reset value of every shadow word after a cold reset under the match. Functional coverage: Phase 1 group `sep_sec_dis_boot_cg` (`cp_hold`, `cp_release`, `cp_rehold`, `cp_map_resp`), `sep_sec_dis_activation_cg` (`cp_match_phase` before-sense and during-sense bins, seed-dependent) and `sep_sec_dis_robustness_cg` (`cp_stops_sense`, seed-dependent, `cp_reset_values_hold`). The logged controls (the retained match after the cold reset, the OKAY map read, the recovery) credit no bin.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the class, the staged image and the disable-vector pair. Print `SEED seed=<n> class=<B/C>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Run the class sequence once: steps 4 to 9 for class B or steps 10 to 12 for class C, then steps 13 and 14. Stage a TEST_DEV image through the preload with non-zero distinct words in the drawn fields (`LC_STATE` excepted) and the drawn vector pair. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0 for the whole leaf. Count the staged non-zero fields as m. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Control sense. Cold reset. From reset release, sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o` on every clock until sense done rises. While sense done is low, issue one frontdoor read of a shadow-map word; the response is SLVERR. If sense done is already high at the response, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. At sense done, record t_sense, the clocks from reset release to the rise of sense done. Record t_first and tl, the clocks of the first and the last change of a staged non-zero shadow word in the probe trace. Read every shadow register through the frontdoor; each staged non-zero field reads non-zero and equals its staged value. Set the window W to 3 times t_sense (the multiple 3 is a test parameter). Match latency. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[7]` through the frontdoor and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Record L, the clocks from the issue of the first token-word write to the rise of `security_disable_o`; if it does not rise, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` until it reads 0x2a and check that `security_disable_o` is 0. Cold reset and wait for sense done.
. Class B only.
