Draw k from k_lo = max(1, t_first - L + 1) to k_hi = tl - L - 1 clocks, so that the match lands after the first and before the last load of a staged non-zero word, or take the k that step 6 sets. If k_lo is greater than k_hi, no try can land inside sense, and the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL reason=window`. Cold reset. Release it. After k clocks, write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[7]` through the frontdoor, then write 0x10000 (go bit 16) to `TOKEN_EOP`.
. Class B only. Wait for the rise of `security_disable_o`. If it does not rise, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. At the rise, read `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the probe. The try is accepted when sense done is low, at least one staged non-zero shadow word reads its staged value and at least one staged non-zero shadow word still reads its reset value. At an accepted try, save the probe sample S and the count n_loaded of staged non-zero words that read their staged value.
. Class B only. A try that is not accepted is retried. Set k to the midpoint of k_lo and k when sense done is high or no staged non-zero word reads its reset value; set k to the midpoint of k and k_hi when no staged non-zero word reads its staged value. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[0]` to `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_I[7]` and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` until it reads 0x2a and check that `security_disable_o` is 0. Cold reset and wait for sense done. Repeat steps 4 and 5, up to three tries in all; after the third try that is not accepted, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Class B only. For W clocks from the rise, sample on every clock: sense done is low, `security_disable_o` is 1 and the probe equals S. Within the window, issue one frontdoor read of a shadow-map word; the response is OKAY.
. Class B only. Through the IC_RESET TDR driver, override `sep_reset_n` to release the SEP reset. Read the SEP reset net; it reads released. For W clocks, sample on every clock: sense done is low, `security_disable_o` is 1 and the probe equals S. Then return the TDR slice to the value of the harness reset state.
. Class B only. Cold reset with the token arrays and the match retained. Release it. Go to step 13.
. Class C only. After the control sense, write the eight words of `TOKEN_ZERO` to the SEC_DIS array through the frontdoor and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor until it reads 0x15 (6'b010101). Wait for the rise of `security_disable_o`; if it does not rise, the leaf fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`.
. Class C only. Cold reset with the token arrays retained. Release it.
. Class C only. For W clocks from reset release, sample on every clock: sense done is low, `security_disable_o` is 1 and every probe word except the `LC_STATE` word equals 0. Within the window, issue one frontdoor read of a shadow-map word; the response is OKAY.
. Reset values under the match. While sense done is low, read every register of the eFuse map through the frontdoor. Each response is OKAY. Each field except `LC_STATE` reads its reset value 0x0, and each of the m staged non-zero fields reads 0. Sample `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` at each response; it is low.
. Recovery. Write the eight words of `TOKEN_OTHER` to the SEC_DIS array through the frontdoor and write 0x10000 to `TOKEN_EOP`. Poll `SEC_DISABLE_TOKEN_MATCH` through the frontdoor until it reads 0x2a (6'b101010); `security_disable_o` is 0. Cold reset. Wait for sense done within 2 times t_sense (the multiple 2 is a test parameter). Read every shadow register and `FEAT_CTRL` through the frontdoor and at `lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o`. The shadow words equal the staged image, with `LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`. Log `OBS-SDIS-RECOVER LOG seed=<n> feat=0x<64b> ctl_mismatch=0x2a ctl_sense_done_cycles=<c> ctl_shadows_eq_staged=<0\|1>` and grade nothing: `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades the recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-SDIS-HOLD
|Under the match, the sensed shadow words do not change over the window. Sense done low, `security_disable_o` at 1 and the OKAY frontdoor shadow-map read are logged controls inside the `PASS` line; `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades them (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and the Shadow Map). In class B the sensed shadow words at `efuse_shadow_probe_o` equal their values at the rise of `security_disable_o` over the window and again after the TDR release, and sense done stays low after the TDR release. In class C the sensed shadow words equal their reset values from reset release over the window (`LC_STATE` word excepted; `hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Reset Persistence). A DUT that senses under the match, loads a word after the match, or restarts sensing at the SEP reset release fails. The class B try is accepted only when at least one staged non-zero word is loaded and at least one is still at reset at the rise (step 5), so the probe holds a loaded word and an unloaded word that a DUT could change. Anchor: `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `security_disable_o` and `efuse_shadow_probe_o` on every clock, and the frontdoor response code. Controls: the control sense shows sense done and the staged non-zero shadow words inside the measured sense time (`ctl_sense_done_cycle`); the probe of the control sense at clock k differs from its probe at sense done, so the same words do change after the class B offset (`ctl_probe_changed=1`); a map read before sense done in the control sense answers SLVERR (`ctl_map_resp`); and in class B the SEP reset net reads released after the TDR step (`sep_rst_released=1`), so the window after it tests a released reset. In class C no read command reaches the bank port `efuse_shim_command_req_o` from reset release over the window; its controls are the read commands that the control sense and the recovery sense show at the tap, and a leaf whose control sense shows no read command at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` fails with `CHK-HOOK FAIL`. Evidence: `CHK-SDIS-HOLD PASS seed=<n> class=<B\|C> t_sense=<c> window=<w> probe_unchanged=<1\|na> shadow_reset_over_window=<1\|na> ctl_sense_done_cycle=<c> ctl_probe_changed=1 ctl_map_resp=SLVERR sep_rst_released=<1\|na> match_lat=<c> k=<c\|na> n_loaded=<n\|na> ctl_sense_done=0 ctl_sdis_o=1 ctl_map_resp=OKAY ctl_sense_cmds=<n\|na>`.
|CHK-SDIS-RESET-VALUES
|After a cold reset under the match, every shadow field reads its described reset value 0x0 through the frontdoor, `LC_STATE` excepted, and each response is OKAY. Sense done low at each response is a logged control that `sep_sec_dis_override_test` grades (`hw/sys/sep/doc/security_disable.adoc`, section Reset Persistence; `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`, reset values). The staged non-zero fields read 0. In class B the cold reset follows an interrupted sense in which at least one staged non-zero word is loaded and at least one is not loaded (step 5, `n_loaded` in the evidence line), so a DUT that keeps the partial words has a loaded word to keep and fails. In class C the cold reset follows a full sense, so a DUT that keeps the sensed words fails. Anchor: the frontdoor readback of every register of the map. Control: the same fields read non-zero in the control sense of the leaf (step 3), so a zero reading is a cleared word and not an unwritten one. Evidence: `CHK-SDIS-RESET-VALUES PASS seed=<n> class=<B\|C> fields=<n> staged_nonzero=<m> n_loaded=<n\|na> all_reset_value=1 ctl_sense_done=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The strap sample edge at the early capture point, the shadow reset value of `LC_STATE`, the upper nibble of `lc_state_o` before sense and the secure scan chain are not claimed (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The revision enable of SEC_DIS keeps its A0 value; the test does not set it. A token match with `secure_tm_req_i` at 1 is not made. The sense stop, the OKAY map read, the feature override, the drop after a mismatch, the sense-done time of the recovery sense, its shadow words and its `FEAT_CTRL` are logged controls here, and `sep_sec_dis_override_test` keeps their grade. The TOKEN_ZERO bind to the SEC_DIS digest is graded in `sep_sec_dis_override_test`; this test shows it only through the rise of `security_disable_o` and the 0x15 readback. The reset override is graded in `sep_sec_dis_reset_reach_test`. The match after sense is graded in `sep_sec_dis_override_test`. The SMC fuse controller is not driven.

'''''

[[sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test]]
===== 6.30 `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: eFuse, lifecycle, SEC_DIS, locked-field

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense with the injected shim response, 3 seeds; every leaf runs the injected sense of leg A at one word class that the seed draws, with one cold reset; tool: both (Verilator and VCS); testlist groups `all` and `cpu_stub`, file `testlists/efuse_lcc.toml`

*Source Module*: `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test`

*BFM / Model*: Frontdoor master, the CPU-side bench master of `lsu_stub_all_live`, with the response code of every access (approved). OTP image preload and the direct OTP word read through the frontdoor read interface (approved). `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the program interface outputs (busy, done, error) (approved). Added hooks, error injects that the test adds to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`: the bank-model sense status inject, `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` with `efuse_sense_status_word_i`, which sets the status bit on the sense response of one word; and the shim response hold `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i`, which withholds the shim response of a program request on the `sep` input `efuse_shim_command_resp_i` while it is high.

*Randomization*: `SepSeededRng` from the run seed (RAND-REP). Dimensions: the word class of w, one per leaf, with weight 2 for each edge class (the first allowed word 10, the last allowed word 254) and weight 1 for each other class (the word of the 32-bit field `CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE` at offset 0x88, and a word drawn from 11 to 253 inside a 256-bit field), the drawn word of the last class, the three spare bits, the program timeout cycles T and the hold multiple m. Trap filters: w in 10 to 254; the staged value of each w and of its w + 1 is non-zero; each spare bit reads 0 in the image and its field is unlocked, and the three bits are different; the timeout cell draws T so that m times T clocks fit the run budget, with m at least 2. `reseed = 3` samples other word classes, drawn words, spare bits, timeout cycles and hold multiples. The word class is seed-dependent: the four classes are graded over a merge of regressions, about 4 regressions, because each of the two lighter classes takes 1 in 6 of the leaves and a regression has 3 leaves.

*Objective*: A shim response whose status bit is set during sensing still advances the sensing word counter and leaves that shadow word unwritten, so it reads its reset value, and sense done still rises. Every other word loads its staged value. A program request that waits for its shim response shows `program_busy` and not `program_done` while it waits with the program timeout disabled, and shows `program_done` with `program_status` 0 and no busy after the response. Sources: `hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc` (section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing, the paragraph on the sensing sequence: a strobe with its status bit set advances the counter and leaves that word unwritten); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc` (the reset value 0x0 of every field except `LC_STATE`); `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc` (registers `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`); `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` (section Fuse Command Protocol, the response status bit). The specification does not state what a program request reports when its timeout fires, what a sense response status does to the lock words or the `LC_STATE` word, or what the last word (255) does with a status (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The timeout cell is logged and not graded, and words 0 to 9 and 255 are not drawn.

*Test Coverage*: Leg A, a sense response with its status bit set at one word w of 10 to 254, one drawn word class per leaf: the first allowed word, the last allowed word, a word of a 32-bit field or a word inside a 256-bit field; the word w reads its reset value, its neighbour w + 1 and every other word read the staged value, and sense done rises. Leg B, the program lifecycle with the program timeout disabled: a program with a normal response ends with done, status 0 and no busy; a program with a withheld response shows busy and not done, and shows done, status 0 and no busy after the release. A timeout cell with the timeout enabled and the response withheld, logged and not graded. `sep_efuse_sense_test` and `sep_efuse_image_test` sense an image with every response normal, `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` drops a program write through the bank-model plusargs, and `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test` grades the read timeout. The open owners keep those cells. Only the new cells are graded here: the sense response with the status bit set and the busy and done lifecycle of a program request whose shim response is late. Functional coverage: `sep_efuse_if_status_cg` (`cp_sense_status_word`, `x_prog_lifecycle`; both fill on every seed).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw from the seed the word class, the word of the drawn class, the three spare bits a, b and c, T and m. The class gives one word w and its neighbour w + 1. Print `SEED seed=<n>` and one `DRAW` line per drawn value. Every wait in the steps below is a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check that the step feeds.
. Stage a TEST_DEV image through the preload with non-zero distinct words in every field of shadow words 10 to 254, every lock bit 0 and the other fields by the image rules. Each of a, b and c sits at a 0 bit of its staged spare word. Hold `secure_tm_req_i` at 0.
. Leg A control sense. With both injects low, cold reset. From reset release, count the clocks until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises; the count is t_sense. Read the probe `efuse_shadow_probe_o`; every word equals its staged value, with `LC_STATE` as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}`. Read words w and w + 1 through the frontdoor; both read their staged value, which is non-zero.
. Leg A injected sense, at the drawn word class. Drive `efuse_sense_status_word_i` = w and `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` = 1. Cold reset. Wait for `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` within a multiple of t_sense (the multiple is a test parameter); a timeout fails CHK-SENSE-STATUS.
. After sense done, read `efuse_shadow_probe_o` for all 256 words. Read words w and w + 1 through the frontdoor. Read `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS` through the frontdoor. Drive `efuse_sense_status_inject_i` = 0.
. Leg B control program. With both injects low, cold reset and wait for sense done. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` with `program_req_timeout_enable` = 0. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of a, `efuse_program_read_back` = 0 and `efuse_program_go` = 1. Poll `program_done` until it reads 1. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`. Read the word of a through the direct read; bit a reads 1.
. Leg B held program. Cold reset and wait for sense done; the program interface registers read their reset values. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` with `program_req_timeout_enable` = 0. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 1. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of b and `efuse_program_go` = 1.
. Poll `efuse_program_busy_o` and `program_busy` until both read 1. Then, for a window of a multiple of t_sense (a test parameter), sample the program interface outputs on every clock and read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` through the frontdoor at least 4 times (a test parameter): `program_busy` is 1 and `program_done` is 0 at every sample and every read.
. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 0. Poll `program_done` until it reads 1. Read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and the program interface outputs. Read the word of b through the direct read; bit b reads 1.
. Timeout cell. Cold reset and wait for sense done. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT` with `program_req_timeout_enable` = 1 and `program_req_timeout_cycles` = T. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 1. Write `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` with `program_enable` = 1, `efuse_data` = 1, `efuse_addr` = the bit address of c and `efuse_program_go` = 1. After m times T clocks, read `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and log `program_busy`, `program_done` and `program_status`. Drive `efuse_shim_resp_hold_i` = 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-SENSE-STATUS
|In the injected sense, sense done rises within the bound, the sensed-shadow output `efuse_shadow_probe_o` and the frontdoor read of word w equal the reset value 0x0, every other word at the probe equals its staged value, and `EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_STATUS.efuse_sense_done` reads 1 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, section Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing; `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_efuse_map.adoc`, reset values; `hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`). A DUT that stops the counter, stores the data of a status word, shifts the following words by one or hangs fails. Anchor: `efuse_shadow_probe_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and the frontdoor readback. Control: the control sense of the same leaf loads word w with its staged value, which is non-zero, so a zero reading comes from the status bit and not from an unwritten word. Evidence: `CHK-SENSE-STATUS PASS seed=<n> word=<w> got_probe=0x0 got_read=0x0 staged=0x<v> others_equal=1 sense_done=1 status_sense_done=1 ctl_word=0x<v>`.
|CHK-PROG-LIFE
|With the program timeout disabled, `program_busy` reads 1 and `program_done` reads 0 over the window; after the release `program_done` reads 1, `program_status` reads 0, `program_busy` reads 0 and the direct read of the word of the programmed bit reads it set (`hw/ip/efuse/regs/gen/adoc/efuse_interface_ctrl.adoc`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL`, `EFUSE_PROGRAM_REQ_TIMEOUT`; `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc`, section Fuse Command Protocol). A DUT that completes without a response, loses the request or reports an error after a late response fails. Anchor: the program interface outputs on every clock, the frontdoor readback of `EFUSE_PROGRAM_CTRL` and the direct read of the programmed word. Control: the same program without a withheld response, in the same leaf from a cold reset, ends with done 1, status 0 and busy 0 and sets its bit at the direct read, so the done bit can read 1 and the direct read can show a burn. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-LIFE PASS seed=<n> bit_addr=<a> held_busy=1 held_done=0 rel_done=1 rel_status=0 rel_busy=0 bit_read=1 ctl_done=1 ctl_bit_read=1`.
|CHK-PROG-TIMEOUT
|A logged cell, not a graded check. The bits `program_busy`, `program_done` and `program_status` after the timeout window are logged as `INFO` and are not graded, because the specification does not state what a program request reports when its timeout fires. Evidence: `CHK-PROG-TIMEOUT INFO seed=<n> cycles=<c> held=<k>x busy=<0\|1> done=<0\|1> status=<0\|1>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CHK-SENSE-STATUS and CHK-PROG-LIFE PASS. UVM_ERROR == 0. CHK-PROG-TIMEOUT logs one `INFO` line, is not counted in `checks=<k>`, and a leaf without that line fails.

*Scope limit*: The test does not claim the report of a program request whose timeout fires, the effect of a sense status on the lock words (words 0 to 2), the `LC_STATE` word (word 3), words 4 to 9 or the last word (word 255), the program with read back, or the response of a read request (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Only the new cells are graded: the open owners `sep_efuse_sense_test`, `sep_efuse_image_test`, `sep_efuse_program_read_back_select_test` and `sep_efuse_illegal_state_fail_closed_test` keep the normal-response sense, the dropped program write and the read timeout.

'''''
